# Chat exports — sorted by theme

[Permalink: Chat exports — sorted by theme](https://github.com/UselessMoron123/all-my-chats/blob/main/chat-themes.md#chat-exports--sorted-by-theme)

All **235 chats** (470 files: each chat has a `.json` and a `.txt` export, paired via their conversation URL) are extracted and sorted into theme folders under `chats/`.

The original archives remain at the repo root if you want them back verbatim.

Folders are numbered by size (01 = biggest). Classification is keyword-based on full titles, so treat it as approximate.

| Folder | Chats | Share | Contents |
| --- | --: | --: | --- |
| `chats/13-Other-stray-questions/` | 5 | 2% | One-off trivia/translation questions that fit no theme. |
| `chats/12-RimWorld/` | 3 | 1% | RimWorld simulator RP + ideal starting pawn stats. |
| `chats/11-Creative-writing/` | 4 | 2% | Monty Python-style dialogue, Roman citizen diary, scene descriptions, text rewriting. |
| `chats/10-Shrek-RP/` | 5 | 2% | Several restarts of a "before chapter 1, not near the swamp" Shrek roleplay. |
| `chats/09-Skyrim-TES-RP/` | 5 | 2% | Skyrim roleplays incl. a Breton isekai and a min-max strategy chat. |
| `chats/08-Meta-about-the-agent/` | 6 | 3% | "What are you", agent capabilities, checking the chat exports themselves, using agent mode from a phone. |
| `chats/07-Spore-RP/` | 7 | 3% | Repeated attempts at a Spore cell-to-space RPG with slow stage progression. |
| `chats/06-Web-forum-fanfic-lookups/` | 10 | 4% | Asking the agent to read pages/forums (Paradox forum, Habr, YouTube), find fanfics, search VK/kubikus, or just reply "прочитал" to pasted text. |
| `chats/05-Game-dev-money/` | 11 | 5% | Solo browser/mobile game ideas, reviving an old wap game, Yandex Games monetization, crypto/AI-agent earning questions. |
| `chats/04-Kenshi/` | 17 | 7% | Route planning from Hub over numbered maps, recruiting lists (free recruits, ≤3000 cost), town checklists, beginner "what do I do first" questions. |
| `chats/03-Game-simulator-RPs/` | 18 | 8% | "Let's roleplay, I want an X simulator" chats: Minecraft, Zomboid, ant colony, cult, Mafia, Harry Potter, peasant sim — plus Sims 4, Stardew Valley, V Rising, Oregon Trail. |
| `chats/02-Original-isekai-RPs/` | 21 | 9% | Original scenario roleplays: medieval/Roman settings, isekai (попаданец) jumps (Pirates of the Caribbean, time travel, Duck Tales), the recurring "Ray" narration series. |
| `chats/01-CK2-CK3/` | 123 | 52% | The dominant theme. Parsing/sorting CK2 trait & character game files, bloodline grouping, councillor/dynasty names, hypotheticals, plus AAR/литстрим writing about dynasties. |

# Chat exports — sorted by theme

All **235 chats** (470 files: each chat has a `.json` and a `.txt` export, paired via their conversation URL) are extracted and sorted into theme folders under `chats/`.

The original archives remain at the repo root if you want them back verbatim.

Folders are numbered by size (01 = biggest). Classification is keyword-based on full titles, so treat it as approximate.

| Folder | Chats | Share | Contents |
|--------|------:|------:|----------|
| `chats/13-Other-stray-questions/` | 5 | 2% | One-off trivia/translation questions that fit no theme. |
| `chats/12-RimWorld/` | 3 | 1% | RimWorld simulator RP + ideal starting pawn stats. |
| `chats/11-Creative-writing/` | 4 | 2% | Monty Python-style dialogue, Roman citizen diary, scene descriptions, text rewriting. |
| `chats/10-Shrek-RP/` | 5 | 2% | Several restarts of a "before chapter 1, not near the swamp" Shrek roleplay. |
| `chats/09-Skyrim-TES-RP/` | 5 | 2% | Skyrim roleplays incl. a Breton isekai and a min-max strategy chat. |
| `chats/08-Meta-about-the-agent/` | 6 | 3% | "What are you", agent capabilities, checking the chat exports themselves, using agent mode from a phone. |
| `chats/07-Spore-RP/` | 7 | 3% | Repeated attempts at a Spore cell-to-space RPG with slow stage progression. |
| `chats/06-Web-forum-fanfic-lookups/` | 10 | 4% | Asking the agent to read pages/forums (Paradox forum, Habr, YouTube), find fanfics, search VK/kubikus, or just reply "прочитал" to pasted text. |
| `chats/05-Game-dev-money/` | 11 | 5% | Solo browser/mobile game ideas, reviving an old wap game, Yandex Games monetization, crypto/AI-agent earning questions. |
| `chats/04-Kenshi/` | 17 | 7% | Route planning from Hub over numbered maps, recruiting lists (free recruits, ≤3000 cost), town checklists, beginner "what do I do first" questions. |
| `chats/03-Game-simulator-RPs/` | 18 | 8% | "Let's roleplay, I want an X simulator" chats: Minecraft, Zomboid, ant colony, cult, Mafia, Harry Potter, peasant sim — plus Sims 4, Stardew Valley, V Rising, Oregon Trail. |
| `chats/02-Original-isekai-RPs/` | 21 | 9% | Original scenario roleplays: medieval/Roman settings, isekai (попаданец) jumps (Pirates of the Caribbean, time travel, Duck Tales), the recurring "Ray" narration series. |
| `chats/01-CK2-CK3/` | 123 | 52% | The dominant theme. Parsing/sorting CK2 trait & character game files, bloodline grouping, councillor/dynasty names, hypotheticals, plus AAR/литстрим writing about dynasties. |

---

### 1. All 40 Previously Missing Agent Mode Chats (+1 New Chat) Are Now Exported

- **`arena-chat-export-txt-2026-09-29T19-30-01-250Z.zip`** (3.25 MB) contains **41 `.txt` files**.
- **`Новая папка.rar`** (4.7 MB compressed / 42.56 MB unpacked) contains **41 `.json` files** (40 `.json` files directly + 1 nested `.rar` containing the 33.8 MB `arena-agent-Модешь поискать в кубикусе...json`).
- **100% of the 40 previously missing Agent Mode chats** from `Новый текстовый документ.txt` are now present in both `.json` and `.txt`, plus the 41st chat (`look in repo. i managed to export my chats, but i suspect it didn't export all of them. check what it missed`).
- Together with your earlier 194 `evaluation` exports, all **234 out of 234** chats from your history list are accounted for.
- The exported `.json` files also confirm the exact root cause of why v2.1 failed on every Agent Mode chat: Arena's raw Agent payload object in the Next.js Flight stream begins with `{"taskId":"chat-agent","triggerAddress":"...","messages":[...]}` rather than `{"messages":[...]}`, which is why v2.1's `flightText.indexOf('{"messages":[')` returned `-1` on 100% of Agent chats.

---

### 2. What Was Still Missing or Affected in the `.txt` vs `.json` Exports

Comparing all 41 `.json` files against all 41 `.txt` files revealed **two issues** (which I have now fixed in `Arena.ai - LMSYS Arena Chat Exporter-2.2.user.js` / `.user.txt` and pushed to PR #1):

#### A. Details Present in `.json` That Were Omitted in `.txt` (`formatAgentPartAsText`)
1. **10 `(empty)` User messages across 7 `.txt` files (`data-tool-results`)**:
   - Whenever the AI used the `ask_user` multiple-choice tool (15 times across your chats), your selected answers and custom responses were saved in the next `user` message under `part.type === "data-tool-results"` (`data[].output.answers`).
   - Because `formatAgentPartAsText` didn't handle `data-tool-results` (or `input.questions` in `tool-ask_user`), the AI's questions only showed `[tool: ask_user]` and **10 of your reply messages rendered as `(empty)` in `.txt`**:
     - `I want a roleplay. Year 2017...` (Messages 3, 5)
     - `Let's roleplay. I want an interactive story_litrpg...` (Messages 3, 5)
     - `Roleplay. I was isekaied in rimworld...` (Messages 3, 5)
     - `Think of a rimworld scenario for me...` (Message 3)
     - `We're going to roleplay as each isekaied into skyrim...` (Message 3)
     - `Давай проведём ролеплей. В стиле подземелий и драконов...` (Message 3)
     - `Я хочу ролеплей в мире марвел...` (Message 3)
2. **35 AI-written files (`tool-write_file`) & 19 file edits (`tool-edit_file`)**:
   - In `.json`, `tool-write_file` contains the full `input.content` of every file the agent created for you (e.g., `/home/user/plan-kripto-zarabotka.md`, `/home/user/All-about-cool-AI/план-заработка.md`, `/home/user/slmp/SLMP_Windows_гайд.md`, `/home/user/slmp/Как_сделать_образ_самому.md`, `/home/user/quintus_channel.md`, `/home/user/summary_miki_billi.md`, `/home/user/character_questionnaires.md`, `/home/user/ducktales/00-ПРАВИЛА-ИГРЫ.md`, `character_sheet.md`, `rimworld_pawns.md`, etc.).
   - In `.txt`, `formatAgentPartAsText` only wrote `[tool: write_file]\npath: ...\nstatus: success` and omitted `input.content` (and `old_text` / `new_text` for `tool-edit_file`).
3. **User file attachments (`part.type === "file"`) & `tool-bash` stdout/stderr**:
   - Two user-uploaded images (`novasurvivalgame_2026-09-17-22-16-21_1789672581690.webp` and `SmallMarked.png`) and `tool-bash` `stdout`/`stderr` outputs were present in `.json` but omitted in `.txt`.

#### B. Next.js Flight `$f0`–`$ff` / `$f00`–`$fff` Hex Reference Bug in 2 Huge Agent `.json` Files
- In `resolveNextFlightReferences`, the reference regex `/^\$(?:L|@|S|F|Q|W)?([0-9a-f]+)$/i` included `F` in the optional prefix group with the case-insensitive `/i` flag.
- Because `f` is also a hexadecimal digit, any Next.js Flight `:T` text chunk whose hex ID had 2+ digits starting with `f` (`$f0`–`$ff` at chunks 240–255, and `$f00`–`$fff` at chunks 3840–4095) had its leading `f` stripped as a prefix:
  - Only **2 of the 41 chats** had enough tool calls (`> 240` large strings) to reach hex ID `$f0`:
    1. `arena-agent-Модешь поискать в кубикусе сообщение от пользователя Ara-2026-09-29T19-22-27-742Z.json` (which has ~1,900 tool calls and ~3,880 `:T` chunks):
       - In Message 3 (parts 51–58, chunks `$f0`–`$ff`) and Message 26 (parts 117–139, chunks `$f00`–`$f39`), stripping the leading `f` turned `$f00`–`$f0f` into `$00`–`$0f` (left unresolved as `"$f00"`–`"$f0f"`) and turned `$f10`–`$f39` into `$10`–`$39`.
       - In particular, in Message 26 Part 133 Result 4, `"$f25"` had its `"f"` stripped to `"25"`, which matched `rowRecords["25"]` (the root Agent payload row itself), embedding a duplicate 5 MB copy of the raw Agent payload inside that search result and bloating the file to 33.8 MB.
    2. `arena-agent-Найди фанфик _ggwp r__ Будущее, человек тестер виртуальн-2026-09-29T19-22-42-016Z.json` (which has 257 large strings):
       - 13 `tool-fetch_page` / `tool-web_search` parts in Messages 7, 9, 11, and 12 (chunks `$f0`–`$ff`) resolved to top-level Flight rows `0`–`f` instead of `:T` text chunks `f0`–`ff`.
  - All other 39 Agent `.json` files had `< 240` `:T` chunks and were completely unaffected.

--- 4 assistant parts 75 ---
[PRESENT_FILE] {'path': 'Arena.ai - LMSYS Arena Chat Exporter-2.2.user.js'}
I inspected your original userscript (`Arena.ai / LMSYS Arena Chat Exporter v2.1`) alongside the export timestamps in your two `.zip` files.

### Why v2.1 Missed All 40 Agent Mode Chats

1. **The 40 missing chats were fetched from `/api/history/unified`, and `fetchAgentById` ran on each of them — but failed during parsing:**
   * You can actually see this in the `exportedAt` timestamps inside your `.zip` files: between exported chat `#1` (line 23 of `Новый текстовый документ.txt`) and exported chat `#2` (line 36), there is a **53.02-second gap** (~4.4 seconds per chat for the 12 Agent Mode chats on lines 24–35). The same ~4.4s gap appears at lines 37, 39, 56, 59, and 61–62.
   * For each of those 40 chats, the script fetched `GET https://arena.ai/agent/<id>` and then threw `unexpected_evaluation_shape`, silently incrementing `failedCount` in `exportBatchConversations`.

2. **Root Cause in `extractAgentPayloadObjectText(flightText)` (lines 275–298 of v2.1):**
   ```js
   const objectStartIndex = flightText.indexOf('{"messages":[', searchIndex);
   ...
   const candidate = JSON.parse(objectText);
   if (
     Array.isArray(candidate?.messages) &&
     candidate.messages.some((message) => Array.isArray(message?.parts) && message?.role)
   )
   ```
   This fails on current Arena Agent Mode pages for three reasons:
   * **Hardcoded first-key assumption (`'{"messages":['`):** v2.1 only looks for a JSON object whose very first property right after `{` is `"messages":[`. As soon as another property precedes `"messages"` in the serialized Next.js Flight props (e.g., `{"session":{...},"messages":[...]}` or `{"id":"...","messages":[...]}` or `{"initialMessages":[...]}`), `"messages":[` is preceded by a comma `,` instead of `{`, so `flightText.indexOf('{"messages":[')` returns `-1`.
   * **Unresolved Flight row references (`$hex`):** In Next.js React Flight (`self.__next_f.push`), `messages`, individual message objects, or `message.parts` can be outlined as Flight row references (e.g. `"messages":"$1a"`, `"messages":["$1b","$1c"]`, or `"parts":"$1d"`). v2.1 only extracted `:T<hex>,` text records and checked `Array.isArray(message?.parts)` *before* resolving Flight references.
   * **Raw `:T<hex>,` text segments left in `flightText` during JSON scanning:** v2.1 scanned `flightText` for JSON braces before stripping out raw `:T<hex>,` text segments, and used a slow per-character `new TextEncoder().encode(char)` loop.

3. **What was missed inside `.txt` exports (`formatEvaluationAsText` & `formatAgentPartAsText`):**
   * In regular evaluation chats, `formatEvaluationAsText` ignored `message.reasoning` (11 messages across 7 of your exported chats have model thinking/reasoning in the `.json` export that was dropped in the `.txt` export).
   * In Agent Mode `.txt` exports, `formatAgentPartAsText` only formatted `query` and `url` tool inputs (ignoring `command`, `path`/`file_path`, `prompt`, etc.) and didn't fall back to `message.content` when `message.parts` was absent.

---

### What I Fixed in `v2.2`

I saved the fixed script to your repository as:
* **`Arena.ai - LMSYS Arena Chat Exporter-2.2.user.js`** (opened in your viewer)
* **`Arena.ai - LMSYS Arena Chat Exporter-2.2.user.txt`**

Key fixes included:
1. **Full Next.js RSC Flight Parser (`parseNextFlightStream` + `resolveNextFlightReferences` + `extractAgentPayloadFromFlight`):**
   * Strips `:T<hex>,` raw text records first (using fast UTF-8 code-point byte counting with row-boundary self-healing), parses all `<id>:<json>` Flight rows, and deeply resolves `$hex` / `$Lhex` / `$@hex` references before inspecting the object tree.
   * Finds `messages` / `initialMessages` regardless of key order inside `{...}`, whether inline or referenced via `$hex`, and falls back between `/agent/<id>`, `/c/<id>`, and `/api/evaluation/<id>`.
2. **"Select agent" Button in the Export Panel:**
   * Added a **`Select agent`** button next to `Select all` and `Clear` so after clicking **`Fetch list`**, you can select **only the 40 Agent Mode chats** if you just want to export the missing ones without re-downloading all 194 regular chats.
3. **Complete `.txt` Export Formatting:**
   * Preserves `[reasoning]` blocks in `.txt` exports for both standard evaluation chats and Agent Mode chats, plus tool inputs (`command`, `path`, `prompt`, `query`, `url`).

### 2. What Was Still Missing or Affected in the `.txt` vs `.json` Exports

Comparing all 41 `.json` files against all 41 `.txt` files revealed **two issues** (which I have now fixed in `Arena.ai - LMSYS Arena Chat Exporter-2.2.user.js` / `.user.txt` and pushed to PR #1):

#### A. Details Present in `.json` That Were Omitted in `.txt` (`formatAgentPartAsText`)
1. **10 `(empty)` User messages across 7 `.txt` files (`data-tool-results`)**:
   - Whenever the AI used the `ask_user` multiple-choice tool (15 times across your chats), your selected answers and custom responses were saved in the next `user` message under `part.type === "data-tool-results"` (`data[].output.answers`).
   - Because `formatAgentPartAsText` didn't handle `data-tool-results` (or `input.questions` in `tool-ask_user`), the AI's questions only showed `[tool: ask_user]` and **10 of your reply messages rendered as `(empty)` in `.txt`**:
     - `I want a roleplay. Year 2017...` (Messages 3, 5)
     - `Let's roleplay. I want an interactive story_litrpg...` (Messages 3, 5)
     - `Roleplay. I was isekaied in rimworld...` (Messages 3, 5)
     - `Think of a rimworld scenario for me...` (Message 3)
     - `We're going to roleplay as each isekaied into skyrim...` (Message 3)
     - `Давай проведём ролеплей. В стиле подземелий и драконов...` (Message 3)
     - `Я хочу ролеплей в мире марвел...` (Message 3)
2. **35 AI-written files (`tool-write_file`) & 19 file edits (`tool-edit_file`)**:
   - In `.json`, `tool-write_file` contains the full `input.content` of every file the agent created for you (e.g., `/home/user/plan-kripto-zarabotka.md`, `/home/user/All-about-cool-AI/план-заработка.md`, `/home/user/slmp/SLMP_Windows_гайд.md`, `/home/user/slmp/Как_сделать_образ_самому.md`, `/home/user/quintus_channel.md`, `/home/user/summary_miki_billi.md`, `/home/user/character_questionnaires.md`, `/home/user/ducktales/00-ПРАВИЛА-ИГРЫ.md`, `character_sheet.md`, `rimworld_pawns.md`, etc.).
   - In `.txt`, `formatAgentPartAsText` only wrote `[tool: write_file]\npath: ...\nstatus: success` and omitted `input.content` (and `old_text` / `new_text` for `tool-edit_file`).
3. **User file attachments (`part.type === "file"`) & `tool-bash` stdout/stderr**:
   - Two user-uploaded images (`novasurvivalgame_2026-09-17-22-16-21_1789672581690.webp` and `SmallMarked.png`) and `tool-bash` `stdout`/`stderr` outputs were present in `.json` but omitted in `.txt`.

#### B. Next.js Flight `$f0`–`$ff` / `$f00`–`$fff` Hex Reference Bug in 2 Huge Agent `.json` Files
- In `resolveNextFlightReferences`, the reference regex `/^\$(?:L|@|S|F|Q|W)?([0-9a-f]+)$/i` included `F` in the optional prefix group with the case-insensitive `/i` flag.
- Because `f` is also a hexadecimal digit, any Next.js Flight `:T` text chunk whose hex ID had 2+ digits starting with `f` (`$f0`–`$ff` at chunks 240–255, and `$f00`–`$fff` at chunks 3840–4095) had its leading `f` stripped as a prefix:
  - Only **2 of the 41 chats** had enough tool calls (`> 240` large strings) to reach hex ID `$f0`:
    1. `arena-agent-Модешь поискать в кубикусе сообщение от пользователя Ara-2026-09-29T19-22-27-742Z.json` (which has ~1,900 tool calls and ~3,880 `:T` chunks):
       - In Message 3 (parts 51–58, chunks `$f0`–`$ff`) and Message 26 (parts 117–139, chunks `$f00`–`$f39`), stripping the leading `f` turned `$f00`–`$f0f` into `$00`–`$0f` (left unresolved as `"$f00"`–`"$f0f"`) and turned `$f10`–`$f39` into `$10`–`$39`.
       - In particular, in Message 26 Part 133 Result 4, `"$f25"` had its `"f"` stripped to `"25"`, which matched `rowRecords["25"]` (the root Agent payload row itself), embedding a duplicate 5 MB copy of the raw Agent payload inside that search result and bloating the file to 33.8 MB.
    2. `arena-agent-Найди фанфик _ggwp r__ Будущее, человек тестер виртуальн-2026-09-29T19-22-42-016Z.json` (which has 257 large strings):
       - 13 `tool-fetch_page` / `tool-web_search` parts in Messages 7, 9, 11, and 12 (chunks `$f0`–`$ff`) resolved to top-level Flight rows `0`–`f` instead of `:T` text chunks `f0`–`ff`.
  - All other 39 Agent `.json` files had `< 240` `:T` chunks and were completely unaffected.

---

### 3. Fixes Applied to `Arena.ai - LMSYS Arena Chat Exporter-2.2.user.js` (and Pushed to PR #1)

1. **Fixed `$fxx` / `$fxxx` Hex Reference Resolution**:
   - Changed the reference matcher in `resolveNextFlightReferences` to `/^\$(?:L|@)?([0-9a-f]+)$/i` (removing `F|S|Q|W` from the prefix group so hex digits `a–f` are never consumed as prefixes), filtered out `I[...]` / `HL[...]` / React VDOM `["$", ...]` rows from `rowRecords`, and seeded `activeStack` with `new Set([rowId])` so no row can ever inline itself.
2. **Complete `.txt` Formatting for All Agent Parts & Attachments**:
   - Added full formatting in `formatAgentPartAsText` for:
     - `data-tool-results` (renders all `ask_user` user answers — verified **0 `(empty)` messages** across all 41 Agent chats),
     - `tool-ask_user` (renders all questions and option labels/descriptions),
     - `tool-write_file` (includes full `content`),
     - `tool-edit_file` (includes `old_text` and `new_text`),
     - `tool-bash` (includes `exit_code`, `stdout`, and `stderr`),
     - `file` parts and `experimental_attachments` (includes filename, media type, and URL).

asset_info=\"$(\n        RELEASE_JSON=\"$release_json\" ARCH_TAG=\"linux-${SEVENZIP_ARCH}\" python3 - <<'PY'\nimport json\nimport os\nimport sys\n\nrelease = json.loads(os.environ[\"RELEASE_JSON\"])\narch = os.environ[\"ARCH_TAG\"]\nfor asset in release.get(\"assets\", []):\n    name = asset.get(\"name\", \"\")\n    if name.startswith(\"7z\") and name.endswith(f\"{arch}.tar.xz\"):\n        digest = asset.get(\"digest\") or \"\"\n        if digest.startswith(\"sha256:\"):\n            digest = digest.split(\":\", 1)[1]\n        if not digest:\n            sys.exit(\"Selected asset has no SHA256 digest\")\n        print(release.get(\"tag_name\", \"\"))\n        print(name)\n        print(asset[\"browser_download_url\"])\n        print(digest)\n        sys.exit(0)\nsys.exit(f\"No 7-Zip {arch} tarball found in the latest release\")\nPY\n    )\"; then\n        return 1\n    fi\n\n    SEVENZIP_VERSION=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$asset_info\" | sed -n '1p')\"\n    ARCHIVE_NAME=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$asset_info\" | sed -n '2p')\"\n    DOWNLOAD_URL=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$asset_info\" | sed -n '3p')\"\n    ASSET_SHA256=\"$(printf '%s\\n' \"$asset_info\" | sed -n '4p')\"\n\n    printf 'Version: %s\\n' \"$SEVENZIP_VERSION\"\n    printf 'Asset: %s\\n' \"$ARCHIVE_NAME\"\n    printf 'URL: %s\\n' \"$DOWNLOAD_URL\"\n    printf 'SHA256: %s\\n' \"$ASSET_SHA256\"\n}\n\nresolve_7zip_asset\n``` ### Download and Test Official 7zz\n```\n7-Zip (z) 26.01 (x64) : Copyright (c) 1999-2026 Igor Pavlov : 2026-04-27\n```\n\nConfirm that the official upstream binary lists RAR and RAR5 extraction handlers:",

asset_json=$(gh api 'repos/ip7z/7zip/releases/latest')
url=$(printf '%s' "$asset_json" | python3 -c 'import json,sys; d=json.load(sys.stdin); print(next(a["browser_download_url"] for a in d["assets"] if a["name"].endswith("linux-x64.tar.xz")))')

curl -fsSL "$url" -o /tmp/chat-archive-review/7z.tar.xz
printf 'Release asset: %s\nExpected digest: %s\nActual digest: sha256:%s\n' "$url" "$digest" "$(sha256sum /tmp/chat-archive-review/7z.tar.xz | cut -d' ' -f1)"
if [ -n "$digest" ] && [ "$digest" != "sha256:$(sha256sum /tmp/chat-archive-review/7z.tar.xz | cut -d' ' -f1)" ]; then echo 'Digest mismatch'; exit 1; fi
tar -xJf /tmp/chat-archive-review/7z.tar.xz -C /tmp/chat-archive-review/sevenzip
find /tmp/chat-archive-review/sevenzip -maxdepth 1 -type f -printf '%f %s bytes\n'

from pathlib import Path
import re, html
root=Path('/tmp/chat-archive-review/extracted')
for f in sorted(root.glob('page*/Arena*.htm')):
    s=f.read_text(errors='replace')
    print('\n===', f.parent.name, '===')
    for pattern,name in [(r'<link[^>]+rel=["\']canonical["\'][^>]*>', 'canonical'),(r'<meta[^>]+property=["\']og:url["\'][^>]*>', 'og:url'),(r'<meta[^>]+name=["\']description["\'][^>]*>', 'description')]:
        m=re.search(pattern,s,re.I)
        print(name+':', m.group(0)[:500] if m else '(none)')
    agents=sorted(set(re.findall(r'https?://arena\.ai/agent/[0-9a-f-]+',s,re.I)))
    cs=sorted(set(re.findall(r'https?://arena\.ai/c/[0-9a-f-]+',s,re.I)))
    print('agent links',len(agents),agents)
    print('c links',len(cs),cs)
    for term in ['look in repo','look at previous versions','01a0ee40','taskId','triggerAddress','chat-agent','exporter','history/unified','messages']:
        print(term, 'count=',s.lower().count(term.lower()))
        if term.lower() in s.lower() and term in ['look in repo','01a0ee40','taskId','triggerAddress','chat-agent']:
            i=s.lower().find(term.lower())
            print(' snippet:',html.unescape(re.sub(r'<[^>]+>',' ',s[max(0,i-120):i+300])).replace('\\n',' ')[:500])
    scripts=re.findall(r'<script[^>]*>(.*?)</script>',s,re.I|re.S)
    print('inline script chars',sum(map(len,scripts)),'scripts',len(scripts))
PY

from html.parser import HTMLParser
from pathlib import Path
import re, html
class P(HTMLParser):
 def __init__(self): super().__init__(); self.skip=0; self.parts=[]
 def handle_starttag(self,t,a):
  if t in {'script','style','noscript','svg'}: self.skip+=1
 def handle_endtag(self,t):
  if t in {'script','style','noscript','svg'} and self.skip: self.skip-=1
 def handle_data(self,d):
  if not self.skip and d.strip(): self.parts.append(d.strip())
root=Path('/tmp/chat-archive-review/extracted')
for f in sorted(root.glob('page*/Arena*.htm')):
 p=P(); p.feed(f.read_text(errors='replace')); text='\n'.join(p.parts)
 print('\n===',f.parent.name,'visible text',len(text),'chars===')
 print('START:\n',text[:2400])

 print('KEY LINES:')

// ==UserScript==
// @name         Arena.ai / LMSYS Arena Chat Exporter
// @name:zh-CN   Arena.ai / LMSYS Arena 聊天导出器
// @namespace    http://tampermonkey.net/
// @version      2.2
// @description  Export arena.ai, lmarena.ai, and legacy LMSYS Arena chats (including Agent Mode) as JSON or TXT with list selection and ZIP packaging
// @description:zh-CN  导出 arena.ai、lmarena.ai 与旧版 LMSYS Arena 聊天记录（含 Agent Mode），支持 JSON、TXT、列表勾选与 ZIP 打包
// @match        https://arena.ai/*
// @match        https://*.arena.ai/*
// @match        https://lmarena.ai/*
// @match        https://*.lmarena.ai/*
// @match        https://chat.lmsys.org/*
// @match        https://arena.lmsys.org/*
// @require      https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/fflate@0.8.2/umd/index.js
// @run-at       document-idle
// @license      GPLv3
// ==/UserScript==

(function () {
  "use strict";

  const LOG_TAG = "[arena-chat-export]";
  const HISTORY_ENDPOINT = "/api/history/unified";
  const EVALUATION_ENDPOINT_PREFIX = "/api/evaluation/";
  const AGENT_PAGE_PREFIX = "/agent/";
  const CHAT_PAGE_PREFIX = "/c/";
  const DEFAULT_HISTORY_PAGE_SIZE = 20;
  const HISTORY_PAGE_GUARD = 200;

output.content:
        } catch (error) {
          warn("failed to decode Next.js flight chunk", error);
        }
        searchFrom = i + 1;
      } else {
        break;
      }
    }
    return chunks;
  }

  function decodeNextFlightTextFromHtml(html) {
    return decodeNextFlightChunksFromHtml(html).join("");
  }

  function utf8ByteLengthOfCodePoint(codePoint) {
    if (codePoint <= 0x7f) return 1;
    if (codePoint <= 0x7ff) return 2;
    if (codePoint <= 0xffff) return 3;
    return 4;
  }

  function readUtf8ByteLengthSegment(text, startIndex, byteLength) {
    let index = startIndex;
    let bytes = 0;
    const len = text.length;
    while (index < len && bytes < byteLength) {
      const codePoint = text.codePointAt(index);
      const charBytes = utf8ByteLengthOfCodePoint(codePoint);
      if (bytes + charBytes > byteLength) {
        break;
      }
      bytes += charBytes;
      index += codePoint > 0xffff ? 2 : 1;
    }

    // Verify if `index` aligns with the next Flight row header; self-heal if off by a few chars
    const nextRowPattern =
      /^(?:\r?\n)?[0-9a-f]{1,6}:(?:T[0-9a-f]+,|[A-Za-z]{0,3}[\[{"\-0-9tfn$])/i;
    if (index < len && !nextRowPattern.test(text.slice(index, index + 24))) {
      const winStart = Math.max(startIndex, index - 32);
      const winEnd = Math.min(len, index + 32);
      const windowStr = text.slice(winStart, winEnd);
      const boundaryMatch = windowStr.match(
        /(?:\r?\n)([0-9a-f]{1,6}:(?:T[0-9a-f]+,|[A-Za-z]{0,3}[\[{"]))|([0-9a-f]{1,6}:T[0-9a-f]+,)/i
      );
      if (boundaryMatch && boundaryMatch.index != null) {
        const leadingNewlineLen = boundaryMatch[0].startsWith("\r\n")
          ? 2
          : boundaryMatch[0].startsWith("\n") || boundaryMatch[0].startsWith("\r")
          ? 1
          : 0;
        index = winStart + boundaryMatch.index + leadingNewlineLen;
      }
    }

    return {
      text: text.slice(startIndex, index),
      endIndex: index,
    };
  }

  /**
   * Parse all `:T<hex>,` text records AND all `<id>:<json>` Flight rows from `flightText`.
   * Also returns `cleanedFlightText` with `:T` raw segments replaced by newlines so raw text
   * inside `:T` chunks can never interfere with JSON scanning.
   */
  function parseNextFlightStream(flightText) {
    const textRecords = {};
    const rowRecords = {};
    const cleanParts = [];
    const anyTPattern = /([0-9a-f]{1,8}):T([0-9a-f]+),/gi;
    let cursor = 0;
    let match;

    while ((match = anyTPattern.exec(flightText))) {
      const matchStart = match.index;
      if (matchStart > cursor) {
        cleanParts.push(flightText.slice(cursor, matchStart));
      }
      const id = match[1].toLowerCase();
      const byteLength = parseInt(match[2], 16);
      const startIndex = anyTPattern.lastIndex;
      const segment = readUtf8ByteLengthSegment(flightText, startIndex, byteLength);
      textRecords[id] = segment.text;
      rowRecords[id] = segment.text;
      cleanParts.push("\n");
      cursor = segment.endIndex;
      anyTPattern.lastIndex = segment.endIndex;
    }

    if (cursor < flightText.length) {
      cleanParts.push(flightText.slice(cursor));
    }

    const cleanedFlightText = cleanParts.join("");
    const lines = cleanedFlightText.split(/\r?\n/);
    for (const rawLine of lines) {
      const line = rawLine.trim();
      if (!line) continue;
      const rowMatch = line.match(/^([0-9a-f]{1,8}):(.*)$/i);
      if (!rowMatch) continue;
      const rowId = rowMatch[1].toLowerCase();
      const rhs = rowMatch[2].trim();
      if (!rhs || rowId in textRecords) continue;
      // Skip client module import rows (`I[...]`), hint rows (`HL[...]`), error/debug rows (`E{...}`)
      if (/^(?:I|HL|E|T|B)\b/.test(rhs) || rhs.startsWith("I[") || rhs.startsWith("HL[")) {
        continue;
      }

      const jsonPayloadMatch = rhs.match(/^([\["{\-0-9tfn].*)$/);
      if (jsonPayloadMatch) {
        try {
          const parsedRow = JSON.parse(jsonPayloadMatch[1]);
          // Skip React element tuples `["$", ...]` at top level when storing data rows
          if (Array.isArray(parsedRow) && parsedRow[0] === "$") {
            continue;
          }
          rowRecords[rowId] = parsedRow;
        } catch (_error) {
          // Ignore non-JSON RSC rows
        }
      }
    }

    return {
      textRecords,
      rowRecords,
      cleanedFlightText,
    };
  }

  function extractJsonBracketedAt(text, startIndex, openChar, closeChar) {
    if (text[startIndex] !== openChar) {
      return null;
    }
    let depth = 0;
    let inString = false;
    let escaping = false;
    for (let index = startIndex; index < text.length; index += 1) {
      const char = text[index];
      if (inString) {
        if (escaping) {
          escaping = false;
        } else if (char === "\\") {
          escaping = true;
        } else if (char === '"') {
          inString = false;
        }
        continue;
      }
      if (char === '"') {
        inString = true;
        continue;
      }
      if (char === openChar) {
        depth += 1;
      } else if (char === closeChar) {
        depth -= 1;
        if (depth === 0) {
          return text.slice(startIndex, index + 1);
        }
      }
    }
    return null;
  }

  function extractJsonObjectAt(text, startIndex) {
    return extractJsonBracketedAt(text, startIndex, "{", "}");
  }

  function extractJsonArrayAt(text, startIndex) {
    return extractJsonBracketedAt(text, startIndex, "[", "]");
  }

  /**
   * Deeply resolves Next.js Flight references (`"$1a"`, `"$L1a"`, `"$@1a"`, `"$$"`, `"$D..."`, etc.)
   * against both `rowRecords` and `textRecords`.
   */
  function resolveNextFlightReferences(value, rowRecords, textRecords, activeStack) {
    const stack = activeStack || new Set();

    if (typeof value === "string") {
      if (value.startsWith("$$")) {
        return value.slice(1);
      }
      if (value === "$undefined") {
        return undefined;
      }
      if (value.startsWith("$D") && value.length > 2) {
        return value.slice(2);
      }
      // Only strip non-hex RSC reference prefixes (`L` or `@`), never `F` (which is a hex digit!)
      const match = value.match(/^\$(?:L|@)?([0-9a-f]+)$/i);
      if (match) {
        const refId = match[1].toLowerCase();
        if (textRecords && typeof textRecords[refId] === "string") {
          return textRecords[refId];
        }
        if (rowRecords && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rowRecords, refId)) {
          const targetRow = rowRecords[refId];
          if (Array.isArray(targetRow) && targetRow[0] === "$") {
            return value;
          }
          if (stack.has(refId)) {
            return value;
          }
          const nextStack = new Set(stack);
          nextStack.add(refId);
          return resolveNextFlightReferences(
            targetRow,
            rowRecords,
            textRecords,
            nextStack
          );
        }
      }
      return value;
    }

    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
      return value.map((item) =>
        resolveNextFlightReferences(item, rowRecords, textRecords, stack)
      );
    }

    if (value && typeof value === "object") {
      const next = {};
      for (const [rawKey, item] of Object.entries(value)) {
        const key = rawKey.startsWith("$$") ? rawKey.slice(1) : rawKey;
        const resolvedItem = resolveNextFlightReferences(
          item,
          rowRecords,
          textRecords,
          stack
        );
        if (resolvedItem !== undefined) {
          next[key] = resolvedItem;
        }
      }
      return next;
    }

    return value;
  }

  function isLikelyAgentMessage(msg) {
    if (!msg || typeof msg !== "object" || Array.isArray(msg)) {
      return false;
    }
    const role = String(msg.role || msg.speaker || msg.author || "").toLowerCase();
    const validRole =
      role === "user" ||
      role === "assistant" ||
      role === "system" ||
      role === "tool" ||
      role === "model" ||
      role === "data" ||
      role === "human" ||
      role === "ai";
    if (!validRole) {
      return false;
    }
    return (
      Array.isArray(msg.parts) ||
      msg.content !== undefined ||
      typeof msg.text === "string" ||
      Array.isArray(msg.toolInvocations) ||
      Array.isArray(msg.tool_calls) ||
      typeof msg.id === "string"
    );
  }

  function isLikelyAgentMessageArray(arr) {
    if (!Array.isArray(arr) || arr.length === 0) {
      return false;
    }
    const validCount = arr.filter(isLikelyAgentMessage).length;
    return validCount > 0 && validCount >= Math.ceil(arr.length * 0.5);
  }

  function normalizeAgentMessage(msg) {
    if (!msg || typeof msg !== "object") {
      return msg;
    }
    if (Array.isArray(msg.parts) && msg.parts.length > 0) {
      return msg;
    }
    const synthesizedParts = [];
    if (typeof msg.reasoning === "string" && msg.reasoning.trim()) {
      synthesizedParts.push({ type: "reasoning", text: msg.reasoning });
    }
    if (typeof msg.content === "string" && msg.content) {
      synthesizedParts.push({ type: "text", text: msg.content });
    } else if (Array.isArray(msg.content)) {
      for (const item of msg.content) {
        if (typeof item === "string") {
          synthesizedParts.push({ type: "text", text: item });
        } else if (item && typeof item === "object") {
          synthesizedParts.push(item);
        }
      }
    } else if (typeof msg.text === "string" && msg.text) {
      synthesizedParts.push({ type: "text", text: msg.text });
    }
    if (synthesizedParts.length > 0) {
      return {
        ...msg,
        parts: synthesizedParts,
      };
    }
    return msg;
  }

  function scoreAgentCandidate(candidate) {
    if (!candidate || typeof candidate !== "object") {
      return -1;
    }
    const msgs = Array.isArray(candidate.messages) ? candidate.messages : [];
    if (!msgs.length) {
      if (candidate.session || candidate.agentSession || candidate.sandboxId) {
        return 1;
      }
      return -1;
    }
    if (!isLikelyAgentMessageArray(msgs)) {
      return -1;
    }
    let score = msgs.length * 10;
    if (msgs.some((m) => Array.isArray(m?.parts))) {
      score += 50;
    }
    if (candidate.session || candidate.title || candidate.id) {
      score += 20;
    }
    return score;
  }

  /**
   * Extract the Agent conversation payload from HTML using multiple strategies:
   * 1. Full RSC Flight row parsing + deep `$hex` reference resolution + object graph walk
   * 2. Property-level `"messages":[...]` / `"initialMessages":[...]` extraction anywhere in `flightText`
   *    (even when `"messages"` is not the first property of `{...}`)
   * 3. Legacy `{"messages":[` object scan
   */
  function extractAgentPayloadFromFlight(flightText) {
    const { textRecords, rowRecords, cleanedFlightText } =
      parseNextFlightStream(flightText);

    const candidates = [];
    const sessionMeta = {};
    const visited = new WeakSet();

    function inspectNode(node) {
      if (!node || typeof node !== "object") {
        return;
      }
      if (visited.has(node)) {
        return;
      }
      visited.add(node);

      if (Array.isArray(node)) {
        if (isLikelyAgentMessageArray(node)) {
          candidates.push({ messages: node });
        }
        for (const item of node) {
          inspectNode(item);
        }
        return;
      }

      if (node.session && typeof node.session === "object" && !Array.isArray(node.session)) {
        Object.assign(sessionMeta, node.session);
      }
      if (typeof node.title === "string" && node.title.trim() && !sessionMeta.title) {
        sessionMeta.title = node.title.trim();
      }

      const msgKeys = [
        "messages",
        "initialMessages",
        "chatMessages",
        "conversationMessages",
        "turns",
      ];
      for (const key of msgKeys) {
        const val = node[key];
        if (Array.isArray(val) && (isLikelyAgentMessageArray(val) || (val.length === 0 && (node.session || node.sandboxId || node.sessionId)))) {
          candidates.push({
            ...node,
            messages: val,
          });
        }
      }

      for (const child of Object.values(node)) {
        inspectNode(child);
      }
    }

    // Strategy 1: Walk all resolved RSC Flight rows
    for (const rowId of Object.keys(rowRecords)) {
      if (rowId in textRecords) continue;
      try {
        const resolvedRow = resolveNextFlightReferences(
          rowRecords[rowId],
          rowRecords,
          textRecords,
          new Set([rowId])
        );
        inspectNode(resolvedRow);
      } catch (_error) {
        // Continue scanning other rows
      }
    }

    // Strategy 2: Scan for `"messages":` or `"initialMessages":` anywhere inside JSON objects
    // (fixes the v2.1 bug where `{"messages":[` had to be the very first key of the object)
    if (candidates.length === 0) {

  async function fetchHistoryList(pageGuard, includeArchived, hooks) {
    const merged = [];
    let cursor = null;
    let currentPage = 0;
    while (currentPage < pageGuard) {
      const nextPageNumber = currentPage + 1;
      hooks?.onPageRequest?.({
        page: nextPageNumber,
        totalCount: merged.length,
      });
      const params = new URLSearchParams();
      params.set("limit", String(DEFAULT_HISTORY_PAGE_SIZE));
      params.set("includeArchived", includeArchived ? "true" : "false");
      if (cursor) {
        params.set("cursor", cursor);
      }
      const requestUrl = `${location.origin}${HISTORY_ENDPOINT}?${params.toString()}`;
      const payload = await fetchJson(requestUrl);
      const batch = Array.isArray(payload?.entries) ? payload.entries : [];
      merged.push(...batch);

      const hasMore = Boolean(payload?.pagination?.hasMore);
      const nextCursor =
        typeof payload?.pagination?.cursor === "string"
          ? payload.pagination.cursor
          : null;
      currentPage += 1;
      hooks?.onPageLoaded?.({
        page: currentPage,
        batchCount: batch.length,
        totalCount: merged.length,
        hasMore,
      });
      if (!hasMore || !nextCursor) {
        break;
      }
      cursor = nextCursor;
    }
    return merged;
  }

  /**
   * Linearly scan HTML for all `self.__next_f.push([1, "..."])` string chunks.
   * Avoids regex backtracking limits on large RSC chunks and tolerates whitespace
   * or multiple statements inside a `<script>` tag.
   */
  function decodeNextFlightChunksFromHtml(html) {
    const source = String(html || "");
    const chunks = [];
    const marker = "self.__next_f.push(";
    let searchFrom = 0;

    while (searchFrom < source.length) {
      const pushIdx = source.indexOf(marker, searchFrom);
      if (pushIdx < 0) {
        break;
      }
      let i = pushIdx + marker.length;
      while (i < source.length && /\s/.test(source[i])) i += 1;
      if (source[i] !== "[") {
        searchFrom = i;
        continue;
      }
      i += 1;
      while (i < source.length && /\s/.test(source[i])) i += 1;
      if (source[i] !== "1") {
        searchFrom = i;
        continue;
      }
      i += 1;
      while (i < source.length && /\s/.test(source[i])) i += 1;
      if (source[i] !== ",") {
        searchFrom = i;
        continue;
      }
      i += 1;
      while (i < source.length && /\s/.test(source[i])) i += 1;
      if (source[i] !== '"') {
        searchFrom = i;
        continue;
      }
      const quoteStart = i;
      i += 1;
      let escaping = false;
      while (i < source.length) {
        const ch = source[i];
        if (escaping) {
          escaping = false;
        } else if (ch === "\\") {
          escaping = true;
        } else if (ch === '"') {
          break;
        }
        i += 1;
      }
      if (i < source.length && source[i] === '"') {
        const rawJsonLiteral = source.slice(quoteStart, i + 1);
        try {
          chunks.push(JSON.parse(rawJsonLiteral));
        } catch (error) {
          warn("failed to decode Next.js flight chunk", error);
        }
        searchFrom = i + 1;
      } else {
        break;
      }
    }
    return chunks;
  }

  function decodeNextFlightTextFromHtml(html) {
    return decodeNextFlightChunksFromHtml(html).join("");
  }

  function utf8ByteLengthOfCodePoint(codePoint) {
    if (codePoint <= 0x7f) return 1;
    if (codePoint <= 0x7ff) return 2;
    if (codePoint <= 0xffff) return 3;
    return 4;
  }

  function readUtf8ByteLengthSegment(text, startIndex, byteLength) {
    let index = startIndex;
    let bytes = 0;
    const len = text.length;
    while (index < len && bytes < byteLength) {
      const codePoint = text.codePointAt(index);
      const charBytes = utf8ByteLengthOfCodePoint(codePoint);
      if (bytes + charBytes > byteLength) {

   * 1. Full RSC Flight row parsing + deep `$hex` reference resolution + object graph walk
   * 2. Property-level `"messages":[...]` / `"initialMessages":[...]` extraction anywhere in `flightText`
   *    (even when `"messages"` is not the first property of `{...}`)
   * 3. Legacy `{"messages":[` object scan
   */
  function extractAgentPayloadFromFlight(flightText) {
    const { textRecords, rowRecords, cleanedFlightText } =
      parseNextFlightStream(flightText);

    const candidates = [];
    const sessionMeta = {};
    const visited = new WeakSet();

    function inspectNode(node) {
      if (!node || typeof node !== "object") {
        return;
      }
      if (visited.has(node)) {
        return;
      }
      visited.add(node);

      if (Array.isArray(node)) {
        if (isLikelyAgentMessageArray(node)) {
          candidates.push({ messages: node });
        }
        for (const item of node) {
          inspectNode(item);
        }
        return;
      }

      if (node.session && typeof node.session === "object" && !Array.isArray(node.session)) {
        Object.assign(sessionMeta, node.session);
      }
      if (typeof node.title === "string" && node.title.trim() && !sessionMeta.title) {
        sessionMeta.title = node.title.trim();
      }

      const msgKeys = [
        "messages",
        "initialMessages",
        "chatMessages",
        "conversationMessages",
        "turns",
      ];
      for (const key of msgKeys) {
        const val = node[key];
        if (Array.isArray(val) && (isLikelyAgentMessageArray(val) || (val.length === 0 && (node.session || node.sandboxId || node.sessionId)))) {
          candidates.push({
            ...node,
            messages: val,
          });
        }
      }

      for (const child of Object.values(node)) {
        inspectNode(child);
      }
    }

    // Strategy 1: Walk all resolved RSC Flight rows
    for (const rowId of Object.keys(rowRecords)) {
      if (rowId in textRecords) continue;
      try {
        const resolvedRow = resolveNextFlightReferences(
          rowRecords[rowId],
          rowRecords,
          textRecords,
          new Set([rowId])
        );
        inspectNode(resolvedRow);
      } catch (_error) {
        // Continue scanning other rows
      }
    }

    // Strategy 2: Scan for `"messages":` or `"initialMessages":` anywhere inside JSON objects
    // (fixes the v2.1 bug where `{"messages":[` had to be the very first key of the object)
    if (candidates.length === 0) {
      const propRegex = /"(?:messages|initialMessages|chatMessages|conversationMessages|turns)"\s*:\s*/g;
      for (const sourceText of [cleanedFlightText, flightText]) {
        let propMatch;
        propRegex.lastIndex = 0;
        while ((propMatch = propRegex.exec(sourceText))) {
          const matchIndex = propMatch.index;
          const valueStart = propRegex.lastIndex;

          // First try to find the enclosing `{...}` object so sibling keys (like `session`) are kept
          let searchBrace = matchIndex;
          while (searchBrace > 0 && matchIndex - searchBrace < 50000) {
            const openBraceIdx = sourceText.lastIndexOf("{", searchBrace - 1);
            if (openBraceIdx < 0) break;
            const objText = extractJsonObjectAt(sourceText, openBraceIdx);
            if (objText && openBraceIdx + objText.length > valueStart) {
              try {
                const rawObj = JSON.parse(objText);
                const resolvedObj = resolveNextFlightReferences(
                  rawObj,
                  rowRecords,
                  textRecords
                );
                inspectNode(resolvedObj);
                if (candidates.length > 0) break;
              } catch (_error) {
                // Keep searching outer braces or fall back to direct value extraction
              }
            }
            searchBrace = openBraceIdx;
          }
          if (candidates.length > 0) break;

          // Direct value extraction right after `"messages":`
          if (sourceText[valueStart] === "[") {
            const arrayText = extractJsonArrayAt(sourceText, valueStart);
            if (arrayText) {
              try {
                const rawArray = JSON.parse(arrayText);
                const resolvedArray = resolveNextFlightReferences(
                  rawArray,
                  rowRecords,
                  textRecords
                );
                if (isLikelyAgentMessageArray(resolvedArray)) {
                  candidates.push({ messages: resolvedArray });
                }
              } catch (_error) {
                // Keep scanning
              }
            }
          } else if (sourceText[valueStart] === '"') {
            const refMatch = sourceText
              .slice(valueStart, valueStart + 20)
              .match(/^"(\$(?:L|@)?[0-9a-f]+)"/i);
            if (refMatch) {
              const resolvedRef = resolveNextFlightReferences(
                refMatch[1],
                rowRecords,
                textRecords
              );
              if (isLikelyAgentMessageArray(resolvedRef)) {
                candidates.push({ messages: resolvedRef });
              }
            }
          }
        }
        if (candidates.length > 0) break;
      }
    }

    if (candidates.length === 0) {
      return null;
    }

    candidates.sort((a, b) => scoreAgentCandidate(b) - scoreAgentCandidate(a));
    const best = candidates[0];
    const normalizedMessages = (Array.isArray(best.messages) ? best.messages : []).map(
      normalizeAgentMessage
    );

    return {
      ...(Object.keys(sessionMeta).length ? { session: sessionMeta } : {}),
      ...best,
      messages: normalizedMessages,
    };
  }

  function getTextFromAgentParts(parts) {
    return (Array.isArray(parts) ? parts : [])
      .filter((part) => part && part.type === "text" && typeof part.text === "string")
      .map((part) => part.text)
      .filter(Boolean)
      .join("\n\n");
  }

  function buildAgentTitle(agent) {
    const explicitTitle = String(agent?.title || agent?.session?.title || "").trim();
    if (explicitTitle) {
      return explicitTitle;
    }
    const firstUser = (Array.isArray(agent?.messages) ? agent.messages : []).find(
      (message) => String(message?.role || "").toLowerCase() === "user"
    );
    return truncateText(
      getTextFromAgentParts(firstUser?.parts) ||
        normalizeContent(firstUser?.content) ||
        "agent chat",
      80
    );
  }

  function sanitizeAgentForExport(agent) {
    if (!agent || typeof agent !== "object") {
      return agent;
    }
    const next = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(agent));
    if (next.session && typeof next.session === "object" && next.session.publicAccessToken) {
      delete next.session.publicAccessToken;
      next.session.publicAccessTokenRedacted = true;
    }
    return next;
  }

  async function fetchAgentById(agentId, metadata) {
    const id = extractConversationId(agentId);
    if (!id) {
      throw new Error(t("invalid_session_id"));
    }

    const candidateUrls = [
      `${location.origin}${AGENT_PAGE_PREFIX}${id}`,
      `${location.origin}${CHAT_PAGE_PREFIX}${id}`,
    ];

    let lastError = null;
    for (const pageUrl of candidateUrls) {
      try {
        const response = await fetch(pageUrl, {
          method: "GET",
          credentials: "include",
          headers: {
            Accept: "text/html,application/xhtml+xml",
          },
        });
        if (!response.ok) {
          const responseText = truncateText(await safeReadText(response), 300);
          const suffix = responseText ? ` - ${responseText}` : "";
          throw new Error(
            t("request_failed", {
              status: response.status,
              statusText: response.statusText,
              suffix,
            })
          );
        }
        const html = await response.text();
        const flightText = decodeNextFlightTextFromHtml(html);
        const resolved = extractAgentPayloadFromFlight(flightText);
        if (!resolved) {
          throw new Error(t("unexpected_evaluation_shape"));
        }
        const agent = {
          id,
          type: "agentic",
          title: String(metadata?.title || "").trim() || undefined,
          createdAt: metadata?.createdAt || resolved?.session?.createdAt || "",
          updatedAt: metadata?.updatedAt || resolved?.session?.updatedAt || "",
          pageUrl: `${location.origin}${AGENT_PAGE_PREFIX}${id}`,
          ...resolved,
        };
        if (metadata?.title && String(metadata.title).trim()) {
          agent.title = String(metadata.title).trim();
        } else {
          agent.title = buildAgentTitle(agent);
        }
        return agent;
      } catch (error) {
        lastError = error;
      }
    }

    throw lastError || new Error(t("unexpected_evaluation_shape"));
  }

  function normalizeContent(content) {
    if (content == null) {
      return "";
    }
    if (typeof content === "string") {
      return content;
    }
    if (Array.isArray(content)) {
      return content
        .map((part) => {
          if (typeof part === "string") {
            return part;
          }
          if (part && typeof part === "object") {
            if (typeof part.text === "string") {
              return part.text;
            }
            if (part.type === "image_url") {
              return `[image] ${part?.image_url?.url || ""}`;
            }
            return JSON.stringify(part, null, 2);
          }
          return String(part);
        })
        .join("\n");
    }

const src = fs.readFileSync('/tmp/chat-archive-review/Arena-exporter-2.2.user.js', 'utf8');
const start = src.indexOf('function decodeNextFlightChunksFromHtml(');
const end = src.indexOf('function getTextFromAgentParts(', start);
if (start < 0 || end < 0) throw new Error('Could not locate parser functions');
const parserSource = src.slice(start, end) + `\n;globalThis.parser = {decodeNextFlightChunksFromHtml, decodeNextFlightTextFromHtml, parseNextFlightStream, extractAgentPayloadFromFlight};`;
const sandbox = {warn: (...args) => console.warn('[parser warning]', ...args)};
vm.createContext(sandbox);
vm.runInContext(parserSource, sandbox, {timeout: 30000});
const api = sandbox.parser;
for (const file of process.argv.slice(2)) {
  const html = fs.readFileSync(file, 'utf8');
  const chunks = api.decodeNextFlightChunksFromHtml(html);
  const flightText = chunks.join('');
  const parsed = api.parseNextFlightStream(flightText);
  const agent = api.extractAgentPayloadFromFlight(flightText);
  console.log('\nFILE', file);
  console.log({htmlBytes: Buffer.byteLength(html), flightChunks: chunks.length, flightTextChars: flightText.length, flightTextBytes: Buffer.byteLength(flightText), textRows: Object.keys(parsed.textRecords).length, jsonRows: Object.keys(parsed.rowRecords).length, agentFound: !!agent});
  if (agent) {
    console.log('meta', Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(agent).filter(([k]) => k !== 'messages')));
    console.log('messageCount', agent.messages.length);
    const roles = {};
    let empty=0, parts=0, partTypes={}, unresolved=0;
    for (const m of agent.messages) {
      const role=String(m.role||m.speaker||'unknown').toLowerCase(); roles[role]=(roles[role]||0)+1;
      if (!Array.isArray(m.parts) || !m.parts.length) empty++;
      for (const p of (m.parts||[])) { parts++; partTypes[p.type||'no-type']=(partTypes[p.type||'no-type']||0)+1; }
    }
    console.log({roles, emptyMessages:empty, partCount:parts, partTypes});
    for (let i=0; i<agent.messages.length; i++) {
      const m=agent.messages[i];
      const texts=(m.parts||[]).filter(p=>p.type==='text').map(p=>p.text||'');
      console.log(`msg ${i+1} role=${m.role||m.speaker} id=${m.id||''} parts=${(m.parts||[]).length} textChars=${texts.reduce((n,s)=>n+s.length,0)} partTypes=${(m.parts||[]).map(p=>p.type).join(',')}`);
    }
  }
}

from pathlib import Path
from html.parser import HTMLParser
import re, hashlib
class P(HTMLParser):
 def __init__(self): super().__init__(); self.skip=0; self.text=[]; self.href=None; self.anchors=[]; self.anchor=[]
 def handle_starttag(self,t,a):
  a=dict(a)
  if t in {'script','style','noscript','svg'}: self.skip+=1
  if t=='a': self.href=a.get('href'); self.anchor=[]
 def handle_endtag(self,t):
  if t in {'script','style','noscript','svg'} and self.skip: self.skip-=1
  if t=='a' and self.href is not None:
   self.anchors.append((self.href,' '.join(self.anchor).strip())); self.href=None; self.anchor=[]
 def handle_data(self,d):
  if not self.skip and d.strip(): self.text.append(d.strip())
  if self.href is not None and d.strip(): self.anchor.append(d.strip())

  function isAgentExportObject(record) {
    return (
      record?.type === "agentic" ||
      (Array.isArray(record?.messages) &&
        record.messages.some((message) => Array.isArray(message?.parts)))
    );
  }

  function formatAgentPartAsText(part) {
    if (!part || typeof part !== "object") {
      return "";
    }
    if (part.type === "text" && typeof part.text === "string") {
      return part.text;
    }
    const reasoningText =
      typeof part.text === "string"
        ? part.text
        : typeof part.reasoning === "string"
        ? part.reasoning
        : "";
    if (part.type === "reasoning" && reasoningText.trim()) {
      return `[reasoning]\n${reasoningText}`;
    }
    if (part.type === "file") {
      const filename = part.filename || part.name || "attachment";
      const mediaType = part.mediaType || part.contentType || "";
      const url = part.url || "";
      return `[file] ${filename}${mediaType ? ` (${mediaType})` : ""}${url ? ` - ${url}` : ""}`;
    }
    if (part.type === "data-tool-results" && Array.isArray(part.data)) {
      const blocks = [];
      for (const entry of part.data) {
        if (!entry || typeof entry !== "object") continue;
        const toolName = entry.tool || "tool";
        const entryLines = [`[tool-result: ${toolName}]`];
        const answers = entry.output?.answers;
        if (Array.isArray(answers) && answers.length > 0) {
          for (const ans of answers) {
            const qId = ans?.questionId || "question";
            const selected = ans?.selectedOptionId;
            const custom = ans?.customResponse;
            const valParts = [];
            if (selected) valParts.push(String(selected));
            if (custom) valParts.push(String(custom));
            entryLines.push(`- ${qId}: ${valParts.join(" / ") || "(no answer)"}`);
          }
        } else if (entry.output !== undefined) {
          entryLines.push(
            typeof entry.output === "string"
              ? entry.output
              : JSON.stringify(entry.output, null, 2)
          );
        }
        blocks.push(entryLines.join("\n"));
      }
      return blocks.join("\n\n");
    }
    const partType = String(part.type || "");
    if (
      partType.startsWith("tool-") ||
      partType === "dynamic-tool" ||
      part.toolInvocation
    ) {
      const invocation = part.toolInvocation || part;
      const toolName = String(
        invocation.toolName || partType.replace(/^tool-/, "") || "tool"
      );
      const lines = [`[tool: ${toolName}]`];
      const input = invocation.input || invocation.args;
      if (input && typeof input === "object") {
        if (input.query) {
          lines.push(`query: ${input.query}`);
        } else if (input.url) {
          lines.push(`url: ${input.url}`);
        } else if (input.command) {
          lines.push(`command: ${input.command}`);
        } else if (input.path || input.file_path) {
          lines.push(`path: ${input.path || input.file_path}`);
        } else if (input.prompt) {
          lines.push(`prompt: ${input.prompt}`);
        }
        if (Array.isArray(input.questions) && input.questions.length > 0) {
          input.questions.forEach((q, qIdx) => {
            lines.push(
              `question ${qIdx + 1} (${q?.id || qIdx + 1}): ${q?.question || ""}`
            );
            if (Array.isArray(q?.options)) {
              q.options.forEach((opt) => {
                const desc = opt?.description ? ` — ${opt.description}` : "";
                lines.push(`  - [${opt?.id || ""}] ${opt?.label || ""}${desc}`);
              });
            }
          });
        }
        if (typeof input.content === "string" && input.content.length > 0) {
          lines.push(`content:\n${input.content}`);
        }
        if (typeof input.old_text === "string" || typeof input.new_text === "string") {
          if (typeof input.old_text === "string") {
            lines.push(`old_text:\n${input.old_text}`);
          }
          if (typeof input.new_text === "string") {
            lines.push(`new_text:\n${input.new_text}`);
          }
        }
      }
      const output = invocation.output || invocation.result;
      if (output && typeof output === "object") {
        if (output.status) {
          lines.push(`status: ${output.status}`);
        }
        if (output.exit_code !== undefined && output.exit_code !== null) {
          lines.push(`exit_code: ${output.exit_code}`);
        }
        if (Array.isArray(output.results)) {
          output.results.slice(0, 8).forEach((result, index) => {
            const title = result?.title || result?.url || `result ${index + 1}`;
            const url = result?.url ? ` - ${result.url}` : "";
            lines.push(`${index + 1}. ${title}${url}`);
          });
        } else if (output.title || output.url) {
          lines.push(
            `${output.title || output.url}${output.url ? ` - ${output.url}` : ""}`
          );
        } else if (output.error || output.message) {
          lines.push(`error: ${output.error || output.message}`);
        }
        if (typeof output.stdout === "string" && output.stdout.trim()) {
          lines.push(`stdout:\n${output.stdout.trimEnd()}`);
        }
        if (typeof output.stderr === "string" && output.stderr.trim()) {
          lines.push(`stderr:\n${output.stderr.trimEnd()}`);
        }
      }
      return lines.join("\n");
    }
    return "";
  }

from pathlib import Path
import json,re,unicodedata,html
from collections import Counter,defaultdict

def norm(s):
 s=html.unescape(s or '')
 s=unicodedata.normalize('NFKC',s).casefold()
 # trailing truncation marks can differ; replace them with nothing
 s=s.replace('…',' ').replace('...',' ')
 s=re.sub(r'[^\w]+',' ',s,flags=re.UNICODE)
 return ' '.join(s.split())
expected=[x.strip() for x in Path('Новый текстовый документ.txt').read_text(encoding='utf-8-sig').splitlines() if x.strip()]
records=[]
for p in Path('chats').rglob('*.json'):
 d=json.loads(p.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
 obj=d.get('agent') or d.get('evaluation') or {}
 title=obj.get('title','')
 if d.get('recordType')=='agentic':
  msgs=obj.get('messages',[])
  if msgs:
   m=msgs[0]
   parts=m.get('parts',[])
   user=' '.join(str(x.get('text','')) for x in parts if x.get('type')=='text')
  else:user=''
 else:user=''
 records.append({'path':str(p),'type':d.get('recordType'),'title':title,'ntitle':norm(title),'user':user,'nuser':norm(user)})
matched=[]; missing=[]
for raw in expected:
 n=norm(raw)
 candidates=[]
 for r in records:
  # titles often are clipped at a prefix, and agent titles may end with ellipsis
  if r['ntitle'] and (n.startswith(r['ntitle']) or r['ntitle'].startswith(n) or n[:min(len(n),70)]==r['ntitle'][:min(len(n),70)]):
   candidates.append(r)
  elif r['nuser'] and len(n)>=35 and (n[:70] in r['nuser'] or r['nuser'][:70] in n):
   candidates.append(r)
 if candidates: matched.append((raw,candidates[0]))
 else: missing.append(raw)
print('original title rows:',len(expected),'matched:',len(matched),'unmatched:',len(missing))
print('unmatched samples:',missing[:12])
print('record types:',Counter(r['type'] for r in records))
# show count by dates inside filenames / check IDs all unique
print('unique chat IDs:',len({json.loads(Path(r['path']).read_text(encoding='utf-8')).get('conversationUrl') for r in records}))

  function formatEvaluationAsText(evaluation) {
    const messages = getReadableTextMessages(evaluation?.messages);
    const lines = [];
    lines.push("============================================================");
    lines.push("arena.ai chat export");
    lines.push("============================================================");
    lines.push(`Title    : ${evaluation?.title || "(untitled)"}`);
    lines.push(`Mode     : ${evaluation?.mode || "unknown"}`);
    lines.push(`Started  : ${formatTextTime(evaluation?.createdAt)}`);
    lines.push(`Updated  : ${formatTextTime(evaluation?.updatedAt)}`);
    lines.push(`Messages : ${messages.length}`);
    lines.push(`URL      : ${location.origin}/c/${evaluation?.id || ""}`);
    lines.push("");

    messages.forEach((message, index) => {
      const speaker = formatSpeakerName(message, evaluation);
      const time = formatTextTime(message?.createdAt);
      const reasoning =
        typeof message?.reasoning === "string" && message.reasoning.trim()
          ? `[reasoning]\n${message.reasoning.trim()}`
          : "";
      const mainContent = normalizeContent(message?.content);
      const combinedContent =
        [reasoning, mainContent].filter(Boolean).join("\n\n") || t("empty_message");
      const attachmentSummary = formatAttachmentSummary(message);

      lines.push("------------------------------------------------------------");
      lines.push(`Message  : ${index + 1}`);
      lines.push(`Speaker  : ${speaker}`);
      lines.push(`Time     : ${time}`);
      if (attachmentSummary) {
        lines.push(`Attach   : ${attachmentSummary}`);
      }
      lines.push("------------------------------------------------------------");
      lines.push(combinedContent);
      if (index !== messages.length - 1) {
        lines.push("");
      }
    });

    return `${lines.join("\n")}\n`;
  }

  function isAgentExportObject(record) {
    return (
      record?.type === "agentic" ||
      (Array.isArray(record?.messages) &&
        record.messages.some((message) => Array.isArray(message?.parts)))
    );
  }

  function formatAgentPartAsText(part) {
    if (!part || typeof part !== "object") {
      return "";
    }
    if (part.type === "text" && typeof part.text === "string") {
      return part.text;
    }
    const reasoningText =
      typeof part.text === "string"
        ? part.text
        : typeof part.reasoning === "string"
        ? part.reasoning

**Короткий вывод:** в опубликованном архиве `all-my-chats` все сессии из исходного списка теперь учтены, включая Agent Mode. Но TXT-экспорты всё ещё теряют часть содержимого, а проверить именно последнюю версию вашего экспортёра по `Desktop.rar` не получилось: в нём страницы для примера, а не результат экспорта.

Я нашёл чат [«look in repo. i managed to export my chats…»](https://arena.ai/agent/01a0ee40-fd55-7ead-a85d-5ea62e03cbe8). В нём разбирались прежняя потеря 40 Agent Mode-сессий и последующие исправления экспортёра.

### Что сошлось

В `all-my-chats/main` я независимо проверил содержимое папки `chats/`:

- **235 JSON и 235 TXT**: 194 обычные сессии и 41 Agent Mode.
- У всех 235 уникальных URL есть пара JSON/TXT; дубликатов и непарных файлов нет.
- Все 234 записи из исходного списка названий нашлись; есть ещё одна дополнительная тестовая сессия.
- Все JSON разбираются, а число сообщений в заголовках TXT совпадает с JSON. Пустых Agent-сообщений и пустых строк-маркеров `(empty)` в TXT не обнаружил.

То есть **сам факт пропуска Agent Mode-сессий в опубликованном архиве устранён**.

### Что всё ещё теряется в TXT

В уже опубликованных TXT и в доступном коде v2.2 формат TXT не сохраняет некоторые данные инструментов, хотя они есть в JSON:

- результаты `fetch_page`: 371 блока, около **2,56 млн символов**;
- содержимое `read_file`: 20 блоков, около **357 тыс. символов**;
- описания результатов `web_search`; кроме того, для 547 вызовов TXT показывает только первые 8 результатов, пропуская **1 070** остальных.

При этом содержимое 35 `write_file` и 48 фрагментов `edit_file` в TXT нашлось; непустые reasoning-блоки тоже присутствуют. Ещё 18 выводов `bash` уже помечены в JSON как обрезанные на стороне инструмента — экспортёр не сможет восстановить то, чего Arena ему не передала. Два вложения представлены ссылками и метаданными, но сами файлы внутрь архива не вложены.

**Практический итог:** JSON выглядит хорошим основным архивом; TXT — удобное, но не полностью без потерь представление.

### Что удалось проверить по `Desktop.rar`

Архив целый: проверка CRC прошла. В нём 3 основные сохранённые HTML-страницы и ресурсы сайта, **но нет JSON/TXT-результатов экспортёра**. На страницах панель экспорта показывает состояние `Ready`, а не завершённый экспорт. Парсер из исторической версии v2.2 не находит в этих сохранённых HTML структурированный Agent payload. Это не доказывает, что живой запрос экспортёра сломан: сохранённая страница после загрузки браузером — не обязательно тот же ответ сервера, который экспортёр получает при запросе с активной сессией.

Самой последней версии скрипта в `chat-archive` нет — там сейчас только `Desktop.rar`; в `all-my-chats/main` скрипта тоже нет, а PR с v2.2 закрыт без слияния. Поэтому **именно вашу последнюю правку я подтвердить не могу**. Для полной проверки пришлите текущий `.user.js` и JSON/TXT, полученные экспортёром для одной из этих Agent-сессий — тогда их можно будет сравнить с исходной страницей.

Файлы в текущей ветке я не менял. Отдельно: `chat-archive` публичный, а в сохранённых страницах видны адрес аккаунта и непрозрачные строки, похожие на токен/сессионные данные. Я не цитирую их и не проверял, действительны ли они; если архив не должен быть публичным, стоит проверить его содержимое и при необходимости отозвать активные сессии.

from pathlib import Path
from collections import Counter,defaultdict
import zipfile,json,re,html,unicodedata
root=Path('/tmp/chat-archive-export-audit/check this out')
zj=next(root.rglob('*json*.zip')); zt=next(root.rglob('*txt*.zip'))
J={};T={}
with zipfile.ZipFile(zj) as z:
 for n in z.namelist(): J[n]=json.loads(z.read(n).decode('utf-8'))
with zipfile.ZipFile(zt) as z:
 for n in z.namelist(): T[n]=z.read(n).decode('utf-8','replace')
def norm(s):
 s=html.unescape(s or '').casefold().replace('…',' ').replace('...',' ')
 return ' '.join(re.sub(r'[^\w]+',' ',s,flags=re.UNICODE).split())
byurlj=defaultdict(list);byurlt=defaultdict(list)
for n,d in J.items(): byurlj[d.get('conversationUrl','')].append((n,d))
for n,s in T.items():
 m=re.search(r'(?m)^URL\s*:\s*(\S+)',s)
 if m: byurlt[m.group(1)].append((n,s))
print('ARCHIVE',zj.name,'and',zt.name)
print('counts',len(J),'JSON,',len(T),'TXT; paired URLs',sum(u in byurlt for u in byurlj),'missing TXT',sum(u not in byurlt for u in byurlj),'missing JSON',sum(u not in byurlj for u in byurlt))
print('JSON record types',dict(Counter(d.get('recordType') for d in J.values())))
print('--- per-session ---')
for url,items in byurlj.items():
 if not url: continue
 n,d=items[0]; typ=d.get('recordType'); obj=d.get('agent') or d.get('evaluation') or {}; msgs=obj.get('messages',[])
 tx=byurlt.get(url,[]); tname,txt=tx[0] if tx else ('(missing)','')
 title=obj.get('title',''); roles=Counter(str(m.get('role','?')).lower() for m in msgs)
 parts=Counter(p.get('type','?') for m in msgs for p in m.get('parts',[]) if isinstance(p,dict))
 declared=re.search(r'(?m)^Messages\s*:\s*(\d+)',txt)
 msg_headers=len(re.findall(r'(?m)^Message\s*:\s*\d+\s*$',txt))
 print(f'{typ:10} id={url.rstrip("/").split("/")[-1]} msgs={len(msgs):3} txt_header={declared.group(1) if declared else "?":>3} txt_blocks={msg_headers:3} bytes={Path("/").joinpath(*[])}',end='')
 print(f' json={len(json.dumps(d,ensure_ascii=False)):7} chars txt={len(txt):7} title={title[:72]!r}')
 if typ=='agentic':
  print('  roles',dict(roles),'parts',dict(parts))
print('--- aggregate agent part/output data ---')
tool_types=Counter(); out_keys=defaultdict(Counter); in_keys=defaultdict(Counter); out_chars=Counter(); truncated=Counter(); all_empty=[]; blank_txt=[]
for url,items in byurlj.items():
 if not items: continue
 n,d=items[0]
 if d.get('recordType')!='agentic': continue
 obj=d.get('agent') or {}; msgs=obj.get('messages',[]); txt=(byurlt.get(url) or [(None,'')])[0][1]
 for mi,m in enumerate(msgs,1):
  if not m.get('parts') and not m.get('content') and not m.get('text') and not m.get('reasoning'): all_empty.append((n,mi,m.get('role')))
  for p in m.get('parts',[]):
   if not isinstance(p,dict):continue
   typ=p.get('type','?'); tool_types[typ]+=1
   inp=p.get('input') or {}; out=p.get('output') or {}
   if isinstance(inp,dict): in_keys[typ].update(inp.keys())
   if isinstance(out,dict):
    out_keys[typ].update(out.keys())
    for k,v in out.items():
     if isinstance(v,str):out_chars[(typ,k)]+=len(v)
    if out.get('stdout_truncated'):truncated['stdout']+=1
    if out.get('stderr_truncated'):truncated['stderr']+=1
   if typ=='tool-write_file':
    v=inp.get('content') if isinstance(inp,dict) else None
    if isinstance(v,str) and v and v not in txt: blank_txt.append(('write_file',n,mi,len(v)))
   if typ=='tool-edit_file':
    for k in ('old_text','new_text'):
     v=inp.get(k) if isinstance(inp,dict) else None
     if isinstance(v,str) and v and v not in txt: blank_txt.append(('edit_file.'+k,n,mi,len(v)))
print('parts by type',dict(tool_types))
print('out keys by type',{k:dict(v) for k,v in out_keys.items()})
print('output char counts',{k:v for k,v in out_chars.items() if k[1] in ('content','stdout','stderr','description')})
print('truncated flags',dict(truncated),'JSON agent empty messages',len(all_empty),'write/edit unique payloads not in paired txt',blank_txt[:12], 'count',len(blank_txt))
print('--- filenames and title mapping to manual pages ---')
for p in sorted(root.glob('page*.txt')):
 s=p.read_text(encoding='utf-8',errors='replace'); first=s.splitlines()[0] if s.splitlines() else ''
 matches=[]
 for url,items in byurlj.items():
  if not items:continue
  n,d=items[0]; obj=d.get('agent') or d.get('evaluation') or {}; title=obj.get('title','')
  if norm(first)[:60] and (norm(first)[:60] in norm(title) or norm(title)[:60] in norm(first)):
   matches.append((url.split('/')[-1],n,title))
 print(p.name,'first=',repr(first[:90]),'matches=',matches[:3])

from pathlib import Path
from collections import Counter,defaultdict
import zipfile,json,re,html,unicodedata
root=Path('/tmp/chat-archive-export-audit/check this out')
zj=next(root.rglob('*json*.zip')); zt=next(root.rglob('*txt*.zip'))
J={};T={}
with zipfile.ZipFile(zj) as z:
 for n in z.namelist(): J[n]=json.loads(z.read(n).decode('utf-8'))
with zipfile.ZipFile(zt) as z:
 for n in z.namelist(): T[n]=z.read(n).decode('utf-8','replace')
byj={d.get('conversationUrl'): (n,d) for n,d in J.items()}
bytxt={}
for n,s in T.items():
 m=re.search(r'(?m)^URL\s*:\s*(\S+)',s)
 if m: bytxt[m.group(1)]=(n,s)
def seen(s,v):
 if not isinstance(v,str) or not v.strip():return True
 if v in s:return True
 v=v.strip()
 if len(v)<80:return False
 return any(v[p:p+80] in s for p in (0,len(v)//2,max(0,len(v)-80)))
checks=Counter();missing=defaultdict(list);char_tot=Counter();examples={};web_n=Counter();web_omitted=0;web_over8=0;reason_n=Counter();trunc=Counter();write_counts=Counter();edit_counts=Counter();ask_user=Counter();empty_lines=0
manual_pages=[]
for p in sorted(root.glob('page*.txt')):
 manual_pages.append((p.name,p.read_text(encoding='utf-8',errors='replace')))
manual_text='\n'.join(s for _,s in manual_pages)
for url,(jn,d) in byj.items():
 typ=d.get('recordType'); obj=d.get('agent') or d.get('evaluation') or {}; msgs=obj.get('messages',[])
 pair=bytxt.get(url)
 if not pair:continue
 tn,txt=pair
 empty_lines+=len(re.findall(r'(?m)^\(empty\)\s*$',txt))
 if typ!='agentic':continue
 for mi,m in enumerate(msgs,1):
  for pi,p in enumerate(m.get('parts',[]),1):
   t=p.get('type'); inp=p.get('input') or {}; out=p.get('output') or {}
   if t=='reasoning':
    val=p.get('text') or p.get('reasoning') or ''
    if val.strip(): reason_n['nonempty_JSON']+=1; reason_n['markers_TXT']+=txt.count('[reasoning]')>0 # fixed below
   if t=='tool-fetch_page' and isinstance(out,dict):
    val=out.get('content')
    if isinstance(val,str) and val.strip():
     checks[(t,'content')]+=1;char_tot[(t,'content')]+=len(val)
     if not seen(txt,val):missing[(t,'content')].append((jn,mi,pi,len(val)))
     if not seen(manual_text,val): missing[(t,'content','manual')].append((jn,mi,pi,len(val)))
   if t=='tool-read_file' and isinstance(out,dict):
    val=out.get('content')
    if isinstance(val,str) and val.strip():
     checks[(t,'content')]+=1;char_tot[(t,'content')]+=len(val)
     if not seen(txt,val):missing[(t,'content')].append((jn,mi,pi,len(val)))
   if t=='tool-bash' and isinstance(out,dict):
    for k in ('stdout','stderr'):
     val=out.get(k)
     if isinstance(val,str) and val.strip():
      checks[(t,k)]+=1;char_tot[(t,k)]+=len(val)
      if not seen(txt,val):missing[(t,k)].append((jn,mi,pi,len(val)))
    if out.get('stdout_truncated'):trunc['stdout']+=1
    if out.get('stderr_truncated'):trunc['stderr']+=1
   if t=='tool-web_search' and isinstance(out,dict):
    results=out.get('results',[]);web_n['calls']+=1;web_n['results']+=len(results)
    if len(results)>8:web_over8+=1;web_omitted+=len(results)-8
    for ri,r in enumerate(results,1):
     if not isinstance(r,dict):continue
     for k in ('title','url','description','snippet','content'):
      v=r.get(k)
      if isinstance(v,str) and v.strip():
       checks[(t,k)]+=1;char_tot[(t,k)]+=len(v)
       if not seen(txt,v):missing[(t,k)].append((jn,mi,pi,ri,len(v)))
   if t=='tool-write_file':
    v=inp.get('content') if isinstance(inp,dict) else None
    if isinstance(v,str) and v:
     write_counts['total']+=1
     if v in txt:write_counts['in_TXT']+=1
   if t=='tool-edit_file':
    for k in ('old_text','new_text'):
     v=inp.get(k) if isinstance(inp,dict) else None
     if isinstance(v,str) and v:
      edit_counts['total']+=1
      if v in txt:edit_counts['in_TXT']+=1
   if t in ('tool-ask_user','data-tool-results'):
    ask_user[t]+=1
# Text summary
print('Text preservation check (full value or three 80-char samples):')
for key,n in sorted(checks.items()):
 print(key,'values=',n,'chars=',char_tot[key],'not-found=',len(missing.get(key,[])))
for key,rows in sorted(missing.items()):
 if rows: print('missing sample',key,rows[:6])
print('web search results',dict(web_n),'calls >8',web_over8,'results beyond first 8',web_omitted)
print('write_file',dict(write_counts),'edit_file',dict(edit_counts),'ask/data parts',dict(ask_user),'truncated stdout/stderr',dict(trunc),'empty placeholder lines',empty_lines)
# Analyze corresponding transcript text overlap without printing content.
print('\nManual pages vs exported txt and JSON session messages:')
for p in sorted(root.glob('page*.txt')):
 s=p.read_text(encoding='utf-8',errors='replace'); first=s.splitlines()[0] if s.splitlines() else ''
 cand=[]
 for url,(jn,d) in byj.items():
  obj=d.get('agent') or d.get('evaluation') or {}; title=obj.get('title','')
  if title and (title.casefold().startswith(first[:45].casefold()) or first.casefold().startswith(title[:45].casefold())):cand.append((url,jn,d))
 if not cand:
  print(p.name,'NO_MATCH');continue
 url,jn,d=cand[0]; tn,tx=bytxt[url];obj=d.get('agent') or d.get('evaluation') or {};msgs=obj.get('messages',[])
 text_parts=[]; all_parts=[]; empty=[]
 for i,m in enumerate(msgs,1):
  for part in m.get('parts',[]):
   if part.get('type') in ('text','reasoning'):
    v=part.get('text') or part.get('reasoning') or ''
    if v.strip():
     text_parts.append(v);all_parts.append(v)
   elif part.get('type','').startswith('tool-'):
    x=json.dumps(part,ensure_ascii=False)
    all_parts.append(x)
 # Find human-facing text/reasoning segments in the manually saved page
 hits=[]; misses=[]
 for i,v in enumerate(text_parts,1):
  if len(v.strip())<40:continue
  if seen(s,v):hits.append(i)
  else:misses.append((i,len(v)))
 # compare user's visible transcript to exported TXT length, no content dump
 print(p.name,'json=',jn,'record=',d.get('recordType'),'messages=',len(msgs),'manual_chars=',len(s),'export_txt_chars=',len(tx),'text/reasoning blocks=',len(text_parts),'blocks present in manual=',len(hits),'not matched=',misses[:10])
