python3 - <<'PY'
import zipfile,json,re,collections,os
from pathlib import Path
root=Path('check this out/export attempt')
jsonzip=next(root.glob('*json*.zip'))
txtzip=next(root.glob('*txt*.zip'))

def read_zip(zpath):
    with zipfile.ZipFile(zpath) as z:
        return {Path(n).name: z.read(n) for n in z.namelist() if not n.endswith('/')}

jfiles=read_zip(jsonzip); tfiles=read_zip(txtzip)
print(f'JSON ZIP entries: {len(jfiles)}; TXT ZIP entries: {len(tfiles)}; JSON manifest: {"manifest.json" in jfiles}; TXT manifest: {"manifest.json" in tfiles}')
json_by_id={}
json_stats=collections.Counter(); tool_types=collections.Counter()
for name,data in jfiles.items():
    if not name.lower().endswith('.json'): continue
    try: wrapper=json.loads(data)
    except Exception: json_stats['invalid_json']+=1; continue
    json_stats['json_files']+=1
    rec=wrapper.get('agent') or wrapper.get('evaluation') or {}
    rid=str(rec.get('id') or '')
    if rid: json_by_id[rid]=rec
    json_stats['record_type_'+str(wrapper.get('recordType','unknown'))]+=1
    messages=rec.get('messages') or []
    json_stats['messages']+=len(messages)
    for m in messages:
      for p in m.get('parts') or []:
        typ=str(p.get('type') or 'unknown'); tool_types[typ]+=1
        if typ.startswith('tool-') or typ=='dynamic-tool': json_stats['tool_parts']+=1
        if typ=='tool-fetch_page' and isinstance((p.get('output') or {}).get('content'),str) and (p['output']['content']): json_stats['fetch_page_bodies']+=1
        if typ=='tool-read_file' and isinstance((p.get('output') or {}).get('content'),str) and (p['output']['content']): json_stats['read_file_bodies']+=1
        if typ=='tool-web_search' and isinstance((p.get('output') or {}).get('results'),list):
          json_stats['web_search_calls']+=1; json_stats['web_search_result_items']+=len(p['output']['results'])
          json_stats['web_search_descriptions']+=sum(1 for r in p['output']['results'] if isinstance(r,dict) and isinstance(r.get('description'),str) and r['description'])
        out=p.get('output') or {}
        if isinstance(out,dict):
          json_stats['stdout_trunc_flags']+=int(out.get('stdout_truncated') is True)
          json_stats['stderr_trunc_flags']+=int(out.get('stderr_truncated') is True)

matched=0; no_match=0; txt_stats=collections.Counter(); missing_fetch=missing_read=missing_web_desc=0; missing_stdout=0; trunc_marked=0
for name,data in tfiles.items():
    if not name.lower().endswith('.txt'): continue
    text=data.decode('utf-8-sig','replace')
    txt_stats['txt_files']+=1
    id_match=re.search(r'/agent/([0-9a-f-]{36})',text,re.I)
    if not id_match:
      no_match+=1; continue
    rid=id_match.group(1); rec=json_by_id.get(rid)
    if not rec: no_match+=1; continue
    matched+=1
    txt_stats['matched_txt_files']+=1
    expected_msgs=len(rec.get('messages') or [])
    m=re.search(r'^Messages\s*:\s*(\d+)\s*$',text,re.M)
    if m and int(m.group(1))==expected_msgs: txt_stats['message_counts_match']+=1
    for message in rec.get('messages') or []:
      for p in message.get('parts') or []:
        typ=str(p.get('type') or '')
        out=p.get('output') or {}
        if typ=='tool-fetch_page' and isinstance(out,dict) and isinstance(out.get('content'),str) and out['content']:
          if out['content'] in text: txt_stats['fetch_bodies_present']+=1
          else: missing_fetch+=1
        if typ=='tool-read_file' and isinstance(out,dict) and isinstance(out.get('content'),str) and out['content']:
          if out['content'] in text: txt_stats['read_file_bodies_present']+=1
          else: missing_read+=1
        if typ=='tool-web_search' and isinstance(out,dict) and isinstance(out.get('results'),list):
          for r in out['results']:
            if isinstance(r,dict) and r.get('description'):
              if r['description'] in text: txt_stats['web_search_descriptions_present']+=1
              else: missing_web_desc+=1
        if typ=='tool-bash' and isinstance(out,dict):
          if out.get('stdout_truncated') is True and 'stdout was truncated before export' in text: trunc_marked+=1
          if isinstance(out.get('stdout'),str) and out['stdout'] and out['stdout'] in text: txt_stats['bash_stdout_present']+=1
    if 'attachment links may expire' in text.lower() or 'links may expire' in text.lower() or 'ссылк' in text.lower(): txt_stats['attachment_warning_present']+=1

print('JSON payload summary:',json.dumps(dict(json_stats),sort_keys=True))
print('Tool part types:',json.dumps(dict(tool_types),sort_keys=True))
print('TXT comparison:',json.dumps({**dict(txt_stats),'matched_agent_id_txt_files':matched,'unmatched_txt_files':no_match,'fetch_bodies_missing':missing_fetch,'read_file_bodies_missing':missing_read,'web_search_descriptions_missing':missing_web_desc,'stdout_truncation_notices_found_for_flags':trunc_marked},sort_keys=True))

  function normalizeLocale(locale) {
    const value = String(locale || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!value) {
      return "";
    }
    if (value.startsWith("zh")) {
      return "zh-CN";
    }
    return "en";
  }

  function getInitialLocale() {
    const browserLocale =
      navigator.language || (Array.isArray(navigator.languages) ? navigator.languages[0] : "");
    return normalizeLocale(browserLocale) || "en";
  }

  let currentLocale = getInitialLocale();

  function t(key, vars) {
    const table = I18N[currentLocale] || I18N.en;
    const fallback = I18N.en;
    let text = table[key] || fallback[key] || key;
    if (!vars) {
      return text;
    }
    return text.replace(/\{([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)\}/g, (_match, name) => {
      const value = vars[name];
      return value == null ? "" : String(value);
    });
  }

  function log(...args) {
    console.log(LOG_TAG, ...args);
  }

  function warn(...args) {
    console.warn(LOG_TAG, ...args);
  }

  function toErrorMessage(error) {
    if (!error) {
      return t("unknown_error");
    }
    if (typeof error === "string") {
      return error;
    }
    if (error instanceof Error) {

  if v and len(v)>12: yield path,v
 elif isinstance(v,dict):
  for k,x in v.items(): yield from leaves(x,f'{path}.{k}' if path else str(k))
 elif isinstance(v,list):
  for i,x in enumerate(v): yield from leaves(x,f'{path}[{i}]')

  for p in msg.get('parts') or []:
   typ=str(p.get('type') or '')
   for val_path,val in leaves(p.get('output') or {}):
    total_by_key[(typ,val_path.split('.')[0])]+=1
    if val in txt: present[(typ,val_path.split('.')[0])]+=1
    else: missing[(typ,val_path.split('.')[0])]+=1

   attach_count+=1; url_count+=bool(a.get('url')); media_count+=bool(a.get('contentType') or a.get('mediaType'))

  function sanitizeFileNamePart(text, maxLength) {
    const raw = String(text || "")
      .replace(/[<>:"/\\|?*\u0000-\u001f]/g, "_")
      .replace(/\s+/g, " ")
      .trim();
    if (!raw) {
      return "untitled";
    }
    const compact = raw.replace(/[. ]+$/g, "");
    return compact.slice(0, maxLength || 60) || "untitled";
  }

  function truncateText(text, maxLength) {
    const source = String(text || "");
    if (source.length <= maxLength) {
      return source;
    }
    return `${source.slice(0, Math.max(0, maxLength - 1))}...`;
  }

  function formatUiTime(text) {
    const value = String(text || "").trim();
    if (!value) {
      return "-";
    }
    const date = new Date(value);
    if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
      return value;
    }
    return date.toLocaleString();
  }

 for n in f.namelist():
  if not n.endswith('.json'): continue
  w=json.loads(f.read(n)); r=w.get('agent') or w.get('evaluation') or {}
  for m in r.get('messages') or []:
   for p in m.get('parts') or []:

     cs['reported_lines_eq_returned']+=outlines==got

    if len(examples)<10: examples.append({'limit':lim,'offset':off,'returned_lines':got,'reported_lines':outlines,'size_present':isinstance(o.get('size'),int)})

// @name         Arena.ai / LMSYS Arena Chat Exporter
// @namespace    http://tampermonkey.net/

// @description  Export arena.ai, lmarena.ai, and legacy LMSYS Arena chats (including Agent Mode) as JSON or detailed TXT with complete tool I/O, list selection, and ZIP packaging

    attachment_warning_txt:

  function t(key, vars) {
    const text = STRINGS[key] || key;
    if (!vars) {
      return text;
    }
    return text.replace(/\{([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)\}/g, (_match, name) => {
      const value = vars[name];
      return value == null ? "" : String(value);
    });
  }

  function log(...args) {
    console.log(LOG_TAG, ...args);
  }

  function warn(...args) {
    console.warn(LOG_TAG, ...args);
  }

  function toErrorMessage(error) {
    if (!error) {
      return t("unknown_error");
    }
    if (typeof error === "string") {
      return error;
    }
    if (error instanceof Error) {
      return error.message || String(error);
    }
    return String(error);
  }

  function extractConversationId(text) {
    const source = String(text || "");
    const match = source.match(UUID_PATTERN);
    return match ? match[1].toLowerCase() : null;
  }

  function getConversationIdFromCurrentUrl() {
    const pathname = String(location.pathname || "");
    const match = pathname.match(
      /\/(?:[a-z]{2}(?:-[A-Z]{2})?\/)?c\/([0-9a-f-]{36})(?:\/|$)/i
    );
    return match ? match[1].toLowerCase() : null;
  }

  function getAgentIdFromCurrentUrl() {
    const pathname = String(location.pathname || "");
    const match = pathname.match(
      /\/(?:[a-z]{2}(?:-[A-Z]{2})?\/)?agent(?:s)?\/([0-9a-f-]{36})(?:\/|$)/i
    );

        const target = event.target;
        if (!(target instanceof Node)) {

    if (
      partType.startsWith("tool-") ||
      partType === "dynamic-tool" ||
      part.toolInvocation
    ) {
      const invocation =
        part.toolInvocation &&
        typeof part.toolInvocation === "object" &&
        !Array.isArray(part.toolInvocation)
          ? part.toolInvocation
          : part;
      const toolName = String(
        invocation.toolName || partType.replace(/^tool-/, "") || "tool"
      );
      const lines = [`[tool: ${toolName}]`];
      const nestedInvocation = invocation !== part;
      const metadataSources = nestedInvocation ? [part, invocation] : [part];
      const excludedKeys = new Set([
        "type",
        "input",
        "args",
        "output",
        "result",
      ]);
      if (nestedInvocation) excludedKeys.add("toolInvocation");
      formatPartMetadata(lines, metadataSources, excludedKeys);

      function appendToolIO(source, prefix) {
        if (!source || typeof source !== "object") return;
        for (const key of ["input", "args", "output", "result"]) {
          if (source[key] === undefined) continue;
          appendStructuredFields(lines, `${prefix}${key}`, source[key]);
        }
      }

      appendToolIO(invocation, nestedInvocation ? "toolInvocation." : "");
      if (nestedInvocation) appendToolIO(part, "part.");
      appendTruncationNotices(lines, part, "part");
      return lines.join("\n");
    }

    // Keep unfamiliar future part types visible too. JSON remains the canonical
    // archive, but TXT should never quietly discard a new part payload.
    const lines = [`[part: ${partType || "unknown"}]`];
    appendTruncationNotices(lines, part, "part");
    lines.push(JSON.stringify(part, null, 2));
    return lines.join("\n");
  }

  function formatAgentAsText(agent) {
    const messages = Array.isArray(agent?.messages) ? agent.messages : [];
    const lines = [];
    lines.push("============================================================");
    lines.push("arena.ai agent chat export");
    lines.push("============================================================");
    lines.push(`Title    : ${agent?.title || "(untitled)"}`);
    lines.push(`Type     : agentic`);
    lines.push(`Started  : ${formatTextTime(agent?.createdAt)}`);
    lines.push(`Updated  : ${formatTextTime(agent?.updatedAt)}`);
    lines.push(`Messages : ${messages.length}`);
    lines.push(
      `URL      : ${
        agent?.pageUrl || `${location.origin}${AGENT_PAGE_PREFIX}${agent?.id || ""}`
      }`
    );
        recordType: "agentic",
        conversationUrl:
          agent?.pageUrl || `${location.origin}${AGENT_PAGE_PREFIX}${agent?.id || ""}`,
        agent,
      };
    }
    return {
      exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      source: location.origin,
      warnings: [t("attachment_warning")],
      recordType: "evaluation",
      conversationUrl: `${location.origin}/c/${record?.id || ""}`,
      evaluation: record,
    };
  }

  function buildFileName(record, extension) {
    const title = sanitizeFileNamePart(record?.title || "chat", 56);
    const time = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, "-");
    const prefix = isAgentExportObject(record) ? "arena-agent" : "arena-chat";
    return `${prefix}-${title}-${time}.${extension}`;
  }

  function downloadBlob(fileName, mimeType, content) {
    const blob = content instanceof Blob ? content : new Blob([content], { type: mimeType });
    const objectUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const anchor = document.createElement("a");
    anchor.href = objectUrl;
    anchor.download = fileName;
    document.body.appendChild(anchor);
    anchor.click();
    anchor.remove();
    window.setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(objectUrl), 1000);
  }

  function buildZipFileName(format) {
    const time = new Date().toISOString().replace(/[:.]/g, "-");
    return `arena-chat-export-${format === "txt" ? "txt" : "json"}-${time}.zip`;
  }

  function getExportPayload(record, format) {
    if (!record || typeof record !== "object" || !record.id) {
      throw new Error(t("unexpected_evaluation_shape"));
    }
    if (format === "json") {
      const fileName = buildFileName(record, "json");
      const jsonText = `${JSON.stringify(buildJsonExportObject(record), null, 2)}\n`;
      return {
        fileName,
        mimeType: "application/json;charset=utf-8",
        textContent: jsonText,
      };
    }
    if (format === "txt") {
      const fileName = buildFileName(record, "txt");
      return {
        fileName,
        mimeType: "text/plain;charset=utf-8",
        textContent: isAgentExportObject(record)
          ? formatAgentAsText(record)
          : formatEvaluationAsText(record),
      };
    }
    throw new Error(t("unknown_export_format", { format }));
  }

  async function exportConversation(conversationId, format) {
    const evaluation = await fetchEvaluationById(conversationId);
    const payload = getExportPayload(evaluation, format);
    downloadBlob(payload.fileName, payload.mimeType, payload.textContent);
    return payload.fileName;
  }

  }

  function buildJsonExportObject(record) {
    if (isAgentExportObject(record)) {
      const agent = sanitizeAgentForExport(record);
      return {
        exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
        source: location.origin,
        warnings: [t("attachment_warning")],
        recordType: "agentic",
        conversationUrl:
          agent?.pageUrl || `${location.origin}${AGENT_PAGE_PREFIX}${agent?.id || ""}`,
        agent,
      };
    }
    return {
      exportedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
      source: location.origin,
      warnings: [t("attachment_warning")],
      recordType: "evaluation",
      conversationUrl: `${location.origin}/c/${record?.id || ""}`,
      evaluation: record,
    };
  }

  function buildFileName(record, extension) {
    const title = sanitizeFileNamePart(record?.title || "chat", 56);

    const prefix = isAgentExportObject(record) ? "arena-agent" : "arena-chat";
    return `${prefix}-${title}-${time}.${extension}`;
  }

      function appendToolCompletenessNotice(source) {
        if (!source || typeof source !== "object") return;
        const sourceToolName = String(source.toolName || toolName)
          .toLowerCase()
          .replace(/[\\s-]+/g, "_");
        const input = source.input !== undefined ? source.input : source.args;
        const output = source.output !== undefined ? source.output : source.result;
        if (!output || typeof output !== "object") return;

        if (sourceToolName === "fetch_page") {
          const chunkIndex = Number(output.chunkIndex ?? input?.chunkIndex);
          const totalChunks = Number(output.totalChunks);
          const hasMoreChunks =
            output.hasMore === true ||
            (Number.isFinite(chunkIndex) &&
              Number.isFinite(totalChunks) &&
              totalChunks > 0 &&
              chunkIndex + 1 < totalChunks);
          if (hasMoreChunks) {
            const chunkLabel =
              Number.isFinite(chunkIndex) &&
              Number.isFinite(totalChunks) &&
              totalChunks > 0
                ? ` (chunk ${chunkIndex + 1} of ${totalChunks})`
                : "";
            lines.push(
              `[notice: fetch_page response has more content${chunkLabel}; this exporter does not retrieve missing chunks]`
            );
          }
        }

        if (sourceToolName === "read_file") {
          const totalLines = Number(output.lines);
          const requestedLimit =
            input?.limit == null ? Number.NaN : Number(input.limit);
          const requestedOffset =
            input?.offset == null ? 1 : Number(input.offset);
          const contentLineCount =
            typeof output.content === "string" && output.content.length
              ? output.content.split(/\\r\\n|\\n|\\r/).length
              : 0;
          const startLine =
            Number.isFinite(requestedOffset) && requestedOffset > 0
              ? Math.floor(requestedOffset)
              : 1;
          const returnedLineCount =
            Number.isFinite(requestedLimit) && requestedLimit > 0
              ? Math.floor(requestedLimit)
              : contentLineCount;
          const endLine = startLine + Math.max(0, returnedLineCount) - 1;
          const hasOmittedEarlierLines = startLine > 1;
          const hasOmittedLaterLines =
            Number.isFinite(totalLines) && totalLines > endLine;
          if (hasOmittedEarlierLines || hasOmittedLaterLines) {
            const lineSummary = Number.isFinite(totalLines)
              ? `lines ${startLine}-${Math.min(endLine, totalLines)} of ${totalLines}`
              : `starting at line ${startLine}`;
            lines.push(
              `[notice: read_file returned an excerpt (${lineSummary}); other lines are only present if captured by another tool call]`
            );
          }
        }
      }

python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import os,zipfile
root=Path('check this out/export attempt')
z=next(root.glob('*json*.zip'))
out=Path(os.environ['SCRATCH'])/'json'
out.mkdir(parents=True)
with zipfile.ZipFile(z) as f:

cat > "$scratch/check.js" <<'JS'
const fs=require('fs'),path=require('path'),vm=require('vm'),assert=require('assert');
const source=fs.readFileSync('Arena.ai - LMSYS Arena Chat Exporter-2.3.user.js','utf8');
assert(!source.includes('zh-CN')&&!source.includes('select-agent')&&!source.includes('Select agent'),'unwanted translation/button removed');
assert(source.includes('zipLib.zipSync(archiveEntries, { level: 6 })'),'ZIP compression enabled');
assert(source.includes('log(`ready v${EXPORTER_VERSION}`)'),'runtime version log corrected');
const fs0=source.indexOf('  function formatAgentPartAsText(part) {');
const fs1=source.indexOf('  function buildJsonExportObject(record) {',fs0);
const fmt={formatTextTime:x=>x||'-',location:{origin:'https://arena.ai'},AGENT_PAGE_PREFIX:'/agent/',normalizeContent:x=>typeof x==='string'?x:'',t:k=>({attachment_warning_txt:'Attachment warning',tool_output_warning_txt:'Tool partial-output warning',empty_message:'(empty)'}[k]||k)};
vm.createContext(fmt);vm.runInContext(source.slice(fs0,fs1)+'\n;globalThis.renderAgent=formatAgentAsText;globalThis.renderPart=formatAgentPartAsText;',fmt,{timeout:30000});
const dir=path.join(process.env.SCRATCH,'json');const files=fs.readdirSync(dir).filter(f=>f.endsWith('.json'));
const stats={records:0,messages:0,fetchBodies:0,readBodies:0,searchItems:0,fetchChunkNotices:0,readExcerptNotices:0,stdoutTruncNotices:0,timestampsRendered:0};
for(const file of files){const w=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(dir,file),'utf8'));const r=w.agent||w.evaluation;if(!r||!Array.isArray(r.messages))continue;stats.records++;const txt=fmt.renderAgent(r);assert(txt.includes('Attachment warning'));assert(txt.includes('Tool partial-output warning'));assert(txt.includes(`Messages : ${r.messages.length}`));
 for(const m of r.messages){stats.messages++;if(m.createdAt)stats.timestampsRendered++;for(const p of m.parts||[]){const out=p.output||p.result||{};const rendered=fmt.renderPart(p);
  if(p.type==='tool-fetch_page'&&out.content){assert(txt.includes(out.content));stats.fetchBodies++;if(rendered.includes('fetch_page response has more content'))stats.fetchChunkNotices++;}
  if(p.type==='tool-read_file'&&out.content){assert(txt.includes(out.content));stats.readBodies++;if(rendered.includes('read_file returned an excerpt'))stats.readExcerptNotices++;}
  if(p.type==='tool-web_search'&&Array.isArray(out.results)){assert(txt.includes(JSON.stringify(out.results,null,2)));stats.searchItems+=out.results.length;}
  if(p.type==='tool-bash'&&out.stdout_truncated===true){assert(rendered.includes('stdout was truncated before export'));stats.stdoutTruncNotices++;}
 }}
}
assert.strictEqual(stats.records,17);assert.strictEqual(stats.fetchBodies,100);assert.strictEqual(stats.readBodies,17);assert.strictEqual(stats.searchItems,280);assert.strictEqual(stats.stdoutTruncNotices,14);

const ns=source.indexOf('  function normalizeExportItems(items) {'), ne=source.indexOf('  function installGui() {',ns);let entries,zipOptions;
const ctx={EXPORTER_VERSION:'2.3',extractConversationId:v=>String(v&&typeof v==='object'?v.id||'':v||''),isAgentHistoryItem:v=>v&&v.type==='agentic',getZipLib:()=>({strToU8:s=>new Uint8Array(Buffer.from(s)),zipSync:(e,o)=>{entries=e;zipOptions=o;return new Uint8Array([1]);}}),fetchExportRecord:async i=>{if(i.id==='bad')throw Error('failed');return {id:i.id,type:i.type,title:i.title||i.id}},getExportPayload:(r,f)=>({fileName:`${r.id}.${f}`,textContent:r.id}),isAgentExportObject:r=>r.type==='agentic',warn:()=>{},wait:async()=>{},buildZipFileName:f=>`batch.${f}.zip`,downloadBlob:()=>{},t:k=>k==='attachment_warning'?'attachments may expire':k==='tool_output_warning'?'tool results are archived as recorded':k};
vm.createContext(ctx);vm.runInContext(source.slice(ns,ne)+'\n;globalThis.runBatch=exportBatchConversations;',ctx);
(async()=>{const result=await ctx.runBatch([{id:'a',type:'evaluation'},{id:'bad',type:'agentic'}],'json');assert.strictEqual(result.successCount,1);assert.strictEqual(result.failedCount,1);assert.strictEqual(zipOptions.level,6);const man=JSON.parse(Buffer.from(entries['manifest.json']).toString());assert.deepStrictEqual([man.selectedCount,man.successCount,man.failedCount],[2,1,1]);assert.strictEqual(man.warnings.length,2);assert(entries['a.json']);console.log(JSON.stringify({...stats,manifest_tests:'PASS',zip_deflate_level:zipOptions.level,agent_only_button_removed:'PASS',Chinese_strings_removed:'PASS'},null,2));})().catch(e=>{console.error(e);process.exitCode=1;});
JS
node "$scratch/check.js"

const fs=require('fs'),path=require('path'),vm=require('vm'),assert=require('assert');
const source=fs.readFileSync('Arena.ai - LMSYS Arena Chat Exporter-2.3.1.user.js','utf8');
const a=source.indexOf('  function formatAgentPartAsText(part) {'),b=source.indexOf('  function buildJsonExportObject(record) {',a);
const c={formatTextTime:x=>x||'-',location:{origin:'https://arena.ai'},AGENT_PAGE_PREFIX:'/agent/',normalizeContent:x=>typeof x==='string'?x:'',t:k=>({attachment_warning_txt:'attachment warning',tool_output_warning_txt:'tool output warning',empty_message:'(empty)'}[k]||k)};

const jd=path.join(process.env.SCRATCH,'json'),files=fs.readdirSync(jd).filter(f=>f.endsWith('.json'));
const recs=new Map();for(const f of files){const w=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(jd,f),'utf8'));const r=w.agent||w.evaluation;recs.set(String(r.id).toLowerCase(),{w,r});}

for(const file of txts){const text=fs.readFileSync(path.join(process.env.SCRATCH,'txt',file),'utf8');const match=text.match(/\/(?:agent|c)\/([0-9a-f-]{36})/i);assert(match,'text record URL');const pair=recs.get(match[1].toLowerCase());assert(pair,'text record matches JSON');stat.txtMatches++;const {w,r}=pair;const full=c.renderAgent(r);assert(full.includes('attachment warning'));if(w.recordType==='agentic')assert(full.includes('tool output warning'));
const mm=full.match(/^Messages\s*:\s*(\d+)\s*$/m);if(mm&&Number(mm[1])===r.messages.length)stat.matchedMessageCounts++;

 if(p.type==='tool-fetch_page'&&out.content){assert(full.includes(out.content));stat.fetchBodies++;if(rendered.includes('fetch_page response has more content'))stat.fetchMoreNotices++;}

 if(p.type==='tool-bash'&&out.stdout_truncated===true){assert(rendered.includes('stdout was truncated before export'));stat.stdoutTruncFlags++;stat.stdoutTruncNotices++;}
}}
}
assert.strictEqual(stat.txtMatches,17);assert.strictEqual(stat.matchedMessageCounts,17);assert.strictEqual(stat.fetchBodies,100);assert.strictEqual(stat.readBodies,21);assert.strictEqual(stat.webSearchItems,280);assert.strictEqual(stat.fetchMoreNotices,63);assert.strictEqual(stat.readExcerptNotices,20);assert.strictEqual(stat.imageMetadataNotices,1);assert.strictEqual(stat.stdoutTruncFlags,15);

import zipfile,zlib,time
from pathlib import Path
for p in [Path('arena-chat-export-json-2026-10-03T14-49-47-108Z.zip'),Path('arena-chat-export-txt-2026-10-03T14-52-35-414Z.zip')]:

 for level in (1,6):
  start=time.perf_counter(); size=sum(len(zlib.compress(b,level)) for b in payload)+22+len(payload)*46; ms=(time.perf_counter()-start)*1000
  print(p.name,{'level':level,'estimated_bytes':size,'original_bytes':p.stat().st_size,'saved_pct':round(100*(1-size/p.stat().st_size),1),'python_cpu_ms':round(ms,1)})

## Summary
- Keep v2.2 unchanged as the baseline; add the refined v2.3.1 exporter.
- Remove the Agent-only selection button and Chinese localization (English-only UI).
- TXT preserves recorded Agent tool inputs/outputs and marks partial `fetch_page` chunks, limited `read_file` excerpts, upstream truncation, and metadata-only image reads. Message timestamps are included when present.
- Selected-history ZIPs include `manifest.json` with selected/success/failed counts and item lists. ZIPs use DEFLATE level 6. Attachment binaries are not downloaded; exports warn that links may expire.
- Tool results are archived as recorded; missing chunks, file ranges, or image bytes are not re-fetched.

## Audit of the newly uploaded v2.3 ZIPs
- Both manifests report 17 selected, 17 successful, 0 failed. The JSON and TXT archives each contain the same 16 Agent records plus one regular chat; all 17 IDs and message counts match (123 messages total).
- The JSON files are valid. The TXT files include all 100 recorded `fetch_page` bodies, all 21 non-empty `read_file` bodies, all 280 search results, and all 15 recorded stdout-truncation notices.
- The data also shows upstream limits: 63 `fetch_page` responses say `hasMore`, 20 of 22 `read_file` calls return an excerpt, one image read contains metadata but no image bytes, and 10 attachment references have URLs but no binary data. These are tool/source limitations, not missing chat records; v2.3.1 makes them explicit.
- The uploaded ZIPs are stored uncompressed (about 5.65 MB JSON / 5.05 MB TXT). DEFLATE level 6 is estimated to reduce these samples to about 1.45 MB / 1.40 MB respectively.

## Validation
- `node --check` passes.
- Replayed v2.3.1 formatting against the new JSON ZIP and matched every chat: 100 fetch bodies, 21 read bodies, 280 search results, 63 chunk notices, 20 excerpt notices, one image-metadata notice, and 15 truncation notices.
- Exercised batch-manifest generation with mixed success/failure; verified counts, warnings, and compression level.

## Test boundary
The uploaded archives were generated by v2.3. The v2.3.1 changes are tested against those JSON records offline; a fresh browser export with v2.3.1 is still needed for an end-to-end check.
EOF
)"

gh api repos/UselessMoron123/chat-archive/pulls/1 -X PATCH -f body="$(cat <<'EOF'
## Summary
- Keep v2.2 unchanged as the baseline; add the refined v2.3.1 exporter.
- Remove the Agent-only selection button and Chinese localization (English-only UI).
- TXT preserves recorded Agent tool inputs/outputs and marks partial `fetch_page` chunks, limited `read_file` excerpts, tool errors, upstream truncation, and metadata-only image reads. Message timestamps are included when present.
- Selected-history ZIPs include `manifest.json` with selected/success/failed counts and item lists. ZIPs use DEFLATE level 6. Attachment binaries are not downloaded; exports warn that links may expire.
- Tool results are archived as recorded; missing chunks, file ranges, or image bytes are not re-fetched.

## Audit of the newly uploaded v2.3 ZIPs
- Both manifests report 17 selected, 17 successful, 0 failed. The JSON and TXT archives each contain the same 16 Agent records plus one regular chat; all 17 IDs and message counts match (123 messages total).
- The JSON files are valid. The TXT files include all 100 recorded `fetch_page` bodies, all 21 non-empty `read_file` bodies, all 280 search results, and all 15 recorded stdout-truncation notices.
- The data also shows upstream limits: 63 `fetch_page` responses say `hasMore`, 20 of 22 `read_file` calls return an excerpt, one image read contains metadata but no image bytes, and 10 attachment references have URLs but no binary data. Two `fetch_page` tool calls report errors; their status/error fields are preserved. These are tool/source limitations, not missing chat records; v2.3.1 makes them explicit.
- The uploaded ZIPs are stored uncompressed (about 5.65 MB JSON / 5.05 MB TXT). DEFLATE level 6 is estimated to reduce these samples to about 1.45 MB / 1.40 MB respectively.

## Validation
- `node --check` passes.
- Replayed v2.3.1 formatting against the new JSON ZIP and matched every chat: 100 fetch bodies, 21 read bodies, 280 search results, 63 chunk notices, 20 excerpt notices, one image-metadata notice, 15 truncation notices, and two tool-error notices.
- Exercised batch-manifest generation with mixed success/failure; verified counts, warnings, and compression level.

## Test boundary
The uploaded archives were generated by v2.3. The v2.3.1 changes are tested against those JSON records offline; a fresh browser export with v2.3.1 is still needed for an end-to-end check.
EOF

I audited the **new v2.3 ZIPs**. Overall, they look good: the JSON and TXT archives contain the same **17 chats**—16 Agent chats and one regular chat—and all 17 message counts match (**123 messages total**. Both manifests report **17 selected, 17 successful, 0 failed**.

The TXT export is now preserving the recorded tool data: all **100** non-empty `fetch_page` bodies, **21** non-empty `read_file` bodies, all **280** web-search results, and all **15** stdout-truncation notices.

There are still limits in the *recorded tool responses*, rather than lost chats:

- **63** `fetch_page` responses say more chunks are available; the exporter can’t recover chunks that weren’t captured in the conversation.
- **20 of 22** `read_file` calls appear to be excerpts selected by `offset`/`limit`. One image read contains only metadata, not image bytes.
- There are **10 attachment references**, but no attachment binaries; their URLs may expire.
- Two `fetch_page` tool calls reported errors. They’re captured in the archives; these are tool-call errors, not failed chat exports.

I updated the exporter to **v2.3.1**: removed the Agent-only button and Chinese localization, added explicit notices for partial chunks, read excerpts, image metadata, and tool errors, and included message timestamps where available. Batch ZIPs now use DEFLATE compression; estimates on these samples are about **5.65 → 1.45 MB** for JSON and **5.05 → 1.40 MB** for TXT.

**Checks passed:** syntax validation and replay of the v2.3.1 formatter against the new JSON ZIP, including checks for those partial-result notices. The v2.2 baseline is unchanged. The updated script is open in the viewer; commit `73a07d9` is pushed and [PR #1](https://github.com/UselessMoron123/chat-archive/pull/1) is updated.

The uploaded ZIPs were generated by v2.3, so a fresh browser export with v2.3.1 is still needed to verify the compressed ZIPs and notices end to end.

const fs=require('fs'),path=require('path'),assert=require('assert');
const jd=path.join(process.env.SCRATCH,'json'),td=path.join(process.env.SCRATCH,'txt');
const records=new Map(); const jfiles=fs.readdirSync(jd).filter(f=>f.endsWith('.json')&&f!=='manifest.json');
const tfiles=fs.readdirSync(td).filter(f=>f.endsWith('.txt')&&f!=='manifest.json');
const stats={jsonRecords:jfiles.length,txtRecords:tfiles.length,matchedIds:0,messageCountsMatched:0,agentRecords:0,evaluationRecords:0,messages:0,fetchBodies:0,readBodies:0,webSearchCalls:0,webSearchResultItems:0,exactWebSearchArrays:0,fetchHasMoreNotices:0,readExcerptNotices:0,imageMetadataNotices:0,stdoutTruncatedFlags:0,stdoutTruncNotices:0,toolErrorResponses:0,toolErrorNotices:0,attachmentWarnings:0,toolOutputWarnings:0,messageTimeLines:0};
const manifests={};
for(const format of ['json','txt']){const f=path.join(process.env.SCRATCH,format,'manifest.json');manifests[format]=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(f,'utf8'));}

assert.deepStrictEqual([manifests.txt.version,manifests.txt.format,manifests.txt.selectedCount,manifests.txt.successCount,manifests.txt.failedCount],['2.3.1','txt',17,17,0]);
for(const f of jfiles){const wrapper=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(jd,f),'utf8'));const record=wrapper.agent||wrapper.evaluation;assert(record&&Array.isArray(record.messages));records.set(String(record.id).toLowerCase(),{wrapper,record});stats.agentRecords+=wrapper.recordType==='agentic';stats.evaluationRecords+=wrapper.recordType==='evaluation';stats.messages+=record.messages.length;if(wrapper.recordType==='agentic')assert(wrapper.warnings.some(x=>String(x).includes('re-run tools')));}
for(const f of tfiles){const text=fs.readFileSync(path.join(td,f),'utf8');const m=text.match(/\/(?:agent|c)\/([0-9a-f-]{36})/i);assert(m,`ID in ${f}`);const found=records.get(m[1].toLowerCase());assert(found,`JSON/TXT ID match ${f}`);stats.matchedIds++;
 const {record}=found;const mm=text.match(/^Messages\s*:\s*(\d+)\s*$/m);assert(mm&&Number(mm[1])===record.messages.length);stats.messageCountsMatched++;if(text.includes('Attachments: files are not downloaded'))stats.attachmentWarnings++;if(text.includes('Tool outputs are preserved as recorded'))stats.toolOutputWarnings++;
 for(const msg of record.messages||[]){if(msg.createdAt&&new RegExp('^Time\\s+:','m').test(text))stats.messageTimeLines++;for(const part of msg.parts||[]){const out=part.output||part.result||{};
  if(part.type==='tool-fetch_page'&&out.content){assert(text.includes(out.content));stats.fetchBodies++;if(out.hasMore===true){assert(text.includes('fetch_page response has more content'));stats.fetchHasMoreNotices++;}}
  if(part.type==='tool-fetch_page'&&(out.status==='error'||out.error)){assert(text.includes('tool call reported an error'));stats.toolErrorResponses++;stats.toolErrorNotices++;}
  if(part.type==='tool-read_file'&&out.content){assert(text.includes(out.content));stats.readBodies++;if(out.lines>0&&((Number(part.input?.offset||1)>1)||out.lines>(Number(part.input?.offset||1)+Number(part.input?.limit||0)-1))){assert(text.includes('read_file returned an excerpt'));stats.readExcerptNotices++;}}
  if(part.type==='tool-read_file'&&out.kind==='image'&&!out.content){assert(text.includes('read_file returned image metadata only'));stats.imageMetadataNotices++;}
  if(part.type==='tool-web_search'&&Array.isArray(out.results)){stats.webSearchCalls++;stats.webSearchResultItems+=out.results.length;assert(text.includes(JSON.stringify(out.results,null,2)));stats.exactWebSearchArrays++;}
  if(part.type==='tool-bash'&&out.stdout_truncated===true){stats.stdoutTruncatedFlags++;assert(text.includes('stdout was truncated before export'));stats.stdoutTruncNotices++;}
 }}
}
assert.strictEqual(stats.matchedIds,17);assert.strictEqual(stats.messageCountsMatched,17);assert.deepStrictEqual([stats.fetchBodies,stats.readBodies,stats.webSearchResultItems,stats.fetchHasMoreNotices,stats.readExcerptNotices,stats.imageMetadataNotices,stats.stdoutTruncatedFlags,stats.toolErrorResponses],[100,21,280,63,20,1,15,2]);
console.log(JSON.stringify({manifests:{json:{selected:manifests.json.selectedCount,success:manifests.json.successCount,failed:manifests.json.failedCount},txt:{selected:manifests.txt.selectedCount,success:manifests.txt.successCount,failed:manifests.txt.failedCount}},...stats},null,2));

import zipfile,json,re,collections
from pathlib import Path
jpath=Path('arena-chat-export-json-2026-10-03T15-22-00-415Z.zip'); tpath=Path('arena-chat-export-txt-2026-10-03T15-23-15-756Z.zip')
with zipfile.ZipFile(jpath) as z:j={Path(n).name:json.loads(z.read(n)) for n in z.namelist() if n.endswith('.json') and n!='manifest.json'}
with zipfile.ZipFile(tpath) as z:t={Path(n).name:z.read(n).decode('utf-8') for n in z.namelist() if n.endswith('.txt') and n!='manifest.txt'}
byid={str((w.get('agent') or w.get('evaluation') or {}).get('id','')).lower():(w,w.get('agent') or w.get('evaluation') or {}) for w in j.values()}
summary=collections.Counter(); absent=[]
for name,text in t.items():
 m=re.search(r'/(?:agent|c)/([0-9a-f-]{36})',text,re.I)
 if not m: continue
 pair=byid.get(m.group(1).lower())
 if not pair: continue
 w,r=pair
 for msg in r.get('messages') or []:
  for p in msg.get('parts') or []:
   if p.get('type')!='tool-read_file':continue
   inp=p.get('input') or {};out=p.get('output') or {}
   if not out.get('content'): continue
   offset=inp.get('offset') or 1;limit=inp.get('limit');total=out.get('lines');content=out.get('content') or ''
   nlines=len(content.splitlines())
   partial=(offset>1) or (isinstance(total,int) and total>((offset-1)+(limit if isinstance(limit,int) and limit>0 else nlines)))
   if partial:
    summary['partial_calls']+=1
    if 'read_file returned an excerpt' in text: summary['files_with_notice']+=1
    else:
     summary['partial_missing_marker']+=1
     absent.append({'offset':offset,'limit':limit,'total':total,'content_lines':nlines,'message_has_other_notice':'Tool outputs are preserved as recorded' in text})
print('counts:',dict(summary));print('first missing conditions (metadata only):',absent[:10])
for label,files in [('fetch',j),('txt',t)]: print(label,'files',len(files))

const fs=require('fs'),path=require('path'),assert=require('assert');
const jd=path.join(process.env.SCRATCH,'json'),td=path.join(process.env.SCRATCH,'txt');
const jsonFiles=fs.readdirSync(jd).filter(f=>f.endsWith('.json')&&f!=='manifest.json');
const txtFiles=fs.readdirSync(td).filter(f=>f.endsWith('.txt')&&f!=='manifest.txt');
const records=new Map();const st={jsonRecords:jsonFiles.length,txtRecords:txtFiles.length,matchedIds:0,messageCountsMatched:0,agentRecords:0,evaluationRecords:0,messages:0,fetchBodies:0,readBodies:0,webSearchCalls:0,webSearchResultItems:0,webSearchArraysExact:0,fetchMoreNotices:0,readExcerptNotices:0,imageMetadataNotices:0,stdoutTruncFlags:0,stdoutTruncNotices:0,toolErrorResponses:0,toolErrorNotices:0,attachmentWarningFiles:0,toolOutputWarningFiles:0,messageTimestampLines:0};
for(const f of jsonFiles){const w=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(jd,f),'utf8'));const r=w.agent||w.evaluation;assert(r&&Array.isArray(r.messages));records.set(String(r.id).toLowerCase(),{w,r});st.agentRecords+=w.recordType==='agentic';st.evaluationRecords+=w.recordType==='evaluation';st.messages+=r.messages.length;assert(w.warnings.some(x=>String(x).includes('expire')));if(w.recordType==='agentic')assert(w.warnings.some(x=>String(x).includes('re-run tools')));}
const mj=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(jd,'manifest.json'),'utf8'));const mt=JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path.join(td,'manifest.json'),'utf8'));
for(const [m,f] of [[mj,'json'],[mt,'txt']]) assert.deepStrictEqual([m.version,m.format,m.selectedCount,m.successCount,m.failedCount],['2.3.1',f,17,17,0]);
for(const f of txtFiles){const text=fs.readFileSync(path.join(td,f),'utf8');const match=text.match(/\/(?:agent|c)\/([0-9a-f-]{36})/i);assert(match);const pair=records.get(match[1].toLowerCase());assert(pair);const {record:r}=pair;st.matchedIds++;
 const mm=text.match(/^Messages\s*:\s*(\d+)\s*$/m);assert(mm&&Number(mm[1])===r.messages.length);st.messageCountsMatched++;
 if(text.includes('Attachments: files are not downloaded'))st.attachmentWarningFiles++;
 if(text.includes('Tool outputs are preserved as recorded'))st.toolOutputWarningFiles++;
 for(const msg of r.messages||[]){if(msg.createdAt&&/^Time\s+:/m.test(text))st.messageTimestampLines++;
  for(const p of msg.parts||[]){const o=p.output||p.result||{};
   if(p.type==='tool-fetch_page'&&o.content){assert(text.includes(o.content));st.fetchBodies++;if(o.hasMore===true){assert(text.includes('fetch_page response has more content'));st.fetchMoreNotices++;}}
   if(p.type==='tool-fetch_page'&&(o.status==='error'||o.error)){assert(text.includes('tool call reported an error'));st.toolErrorResponses++;st.toolErrorNotices++;}
   if(p.type==='tool-read_file'&&o.content){assert(text.includes(o.content));st.readBodies++;
    const total=o.lines==null?NaN:Number(o.lines),lim=p.input?.limit==null?NaN:Number(p.input.limit),off=p.input?.offset==null?1:Number(p.input.offset),nlines=o.content?o.content.split(/\r\n|\n|\r/).length:0,start=Number.isFinite(off)&&off>0?Math.floor(off):1,count=Number.isFinite(lim)&&lim>0?Math.floor(lim):nlines,end=start+Math.max(0,count)-1;
    if(start>1||(Number.isFinite(total)&&total>end)){assert(text.includes('read_file returned an excerpt'));st.readExcerptNotices++;}
   }
   if(p.type==='tool-read_file'&&o.kind==='image'&&!o.content){assert(text.includes('read_file returned image metadata only'));st.imageMetadataNotices++;}
   if(p.type==='tool-web_search'&&Array.isArray(o.results)){st.webSearchCalls++;st.webSearchResultItems+=o.results.length;assert(text.includes(JSON.stringify(o.results,null,2)));st.webSearchArraysExact++;}

  }

}

## Summary
- Keep v2.2 unchanged as the baseline; add the refined v2.3.1 exporter.
- Remove the Agent-only selection button and Chinese localization (English-only UI).
- TXT preserves recorded Agent tool inputs/outputs and marks partial `fetch_page` chunks, limited `read_file` excerpts, tool errors, upstream truncation, and metadata-only image reads. Message timestamps are included when present.
- Selected-history ZIPs include `manifest.json` with selected/success/failed counts and item lists. ZIPs use DEFLATE level 6. Attachment binaries are not downloaded; exports warn that links may expire.
- Tool results are archived as recorded; missing chunks, file ranges, or image bytes are not re-fetched.

## Audit of the newly uploaded v2.3.1 ZIPs
- Both manifests report 17 selected, 17 successful, 0 failed. The JSON and TXT archives each contain the same 16 Agent records plus one regular chat; all 17 IDs and message counts match (125 messages total).
- All JSON records are valid. TXT includes all 100 recorded `fetch_page` bodies, all 21 non-empty `read_file` bodies, all 280 search results, and all 15 stdout-truncation notices.
- All expected completeness notices are present: 63 `fetch_page` responses with `hasMore`, 20 limited `read_file` excerpts, one image read with metadata but no bytes, and two tool-call errors. There are also 10 attachment references without binary data; attachment warnings are included.
- The uploaded ZIPs are actually DEFLATE-compressed: JSON is 1.54 MB (5.94 MB uncompressed) and TXT is 1.49 MB (5.30 MB uncompressed), about 74% and 72% smaller respectively.

- `node --check` passes.
- Checked the real v2.3.1 ZIP contents against their manifests and cross-matched every JSON/TXT record, ID, and message count.
- Verified the actual TXT contains the complete serialized search-result arrays plus the fetched/read content and all partial/error markers.
- The v2.2 baseline remains unchanged.
EOF

Confirmed—the new ZIPs were generated by **v2.3.1**, and the actual exports check out.

- Both manifests report **17 selected, 17 successful, 0 failed**. Each ZIP has 17 chat files plus `manifest.json`; all JSON records are valid, and all 17 JSON/TXT pairs match by chat ID and message count (**125 messages total**).
- TXT preserves all **100** recorded `fetch_page` bodies, **21** non-empty `read_file` bodies, all **280** web-search results, and all **15** stdout-truncation notices.
- The new notices are present too: **63** partial `fetch_page` responses, **20** `read_file` excerpts, one image read with metadata but no bytes, and two tool-call errors. These are limitations or errors in the recorded tool results—not failed chat exports.
- The ZIPs use DEFLATE compression: JSON is **1.54 MB** from **5.94 MB** uncompressed; TXT is **1.49 MB** from **5.30 MB**. The attachment warning is included.

**Conclusion:** no missing chat records or JSON/TXT mismatches found. What isn’t present is source data the tools didn’t return—remaining page chunks, unrequested file lines, image bytes, and attachment binaries. The current notices make those limits visible rather than implying they were recovered. I updated PR #1 with this audit.
